# FORCITIS one modernization — session rules

## Baseline

1. Old code, the legacy system and outside systems are evidence only: never copy their structure or names; reproduce legacy behaviour only on production evidence.
2. Never write or print secrets; no production writes; row-level and personal data stay out of sessions and the repo, only aggregates.
3. Follow what the user decided; if you disagree, stop and report. Never decide for the user.
4. When a hook or CI blocks you, fix what it says; never bypass it, loosen or delete an assertion to pass, or change acceptance tests you implement against.
5. Claim done only with reproducible verification; check the source before quoting and mark claims verified or unverified.
6. Every session talks to the user in Chinese, including reports and questions; files agents read and messages between sessions stay English.

## Delivery

Follow `docs/rules/playbooks/<type>.md` for the card's type and report what it lists.

1. Work only in your own worktree and branch, never the main checkout.
2. Run `scripts/ci/check_all.sh` and affected integration tests; if acceptance tests changed, all guard classes too.
3. Dispatch a fresh reviewer per `scripts/review/PROMPT.md`; fix every 必改.
4. PR first line `Closes #<card>` if it finishes the card, else `Refs #<card>`.
5. Enqueue it yourself: `python3 scripts/merge/mq.py enqueue <PR>`.

When unsure: `docs/rules/rules.md`, `docs/rules/roles/<role>.md` (roles: `policy.json` `sessions`).

# Compact instructions

- Keep: card numbers, PR numbers and heads, open items, approaches ruled out and why.
- Drop: command output, file contents, how solved problems were solved.
# backend

`docs/rules/coding-core.md` loads here and is the must-read. Details by id:
- Code: `docs/rules/coding-standard.md` (Java §2, tests §5, pre-PR §7)
- Naming, packages, HTTP: `docs/rules/naming-standard.md` §2, §3, §2.6
- DDL and `src/main/resources/db/migration/`: naming §2.4–2.4a, coding §3, data design per `docs/rules/data-design-checklist.md` §14.1 (migration-in §14.2, outside integration §14.3); table changes need design's sign-off first
- New words: `docs/glossary/terms.yaml`, else register a proposed term (`docs/glossary/CLAUDE.md`)
- Test containers: only images in `backend/architecture/test-images.txt`

# Coding core — FORCITIS one

Loaded for `backend/**` via the `.claude/rules` symlink (#827).
CS-x.y is in `docs/rules/coding-standard.md`, which wins on conflict.

## A. Golden files — copy these

| Part | Class#member | Pattern |
|---|---|---|
| Use case | `SubmitTimesheet#execute` | `@Transactional` on execute, one aggregate, save then publish |
| Caller | `SubmitTimesheet#currentUser` | inject `CurrentUser`, never take `UserId caller` (`HandlerCallerGuardTest`) |
| Data scope | `GetOrgUnit#execute` | inject `OrgUnitAccess`; out of scope = not found + audit |
| Controller | `OrgUnitController#get` | convert, call the use case; `*Response` record on 200, `ResponseEntity` only for other status or headers |
| Repository | `JdbcRoleRepository#update` | text-block lowercase SQL, named params, versioned update |
| Listener | `ApplyApprovalResult#on` | `@ApplicationModuleListener` in application, dedupe first |
| Time | `JdbcRoleRepository#at` | `Instant` + `BusinessZone`; JDBC `OffsetDateTime`, not `Timestamp` |
| ID | `CreateLegalEntity#execute` | inject `IdGenerator` |
| Money | `ExactMoney#round` | compute unrounded in `ExactMoney`; `round()` to `Money`, HALF_UP to the minor unit; total = sum of rounded lines (FD-02/07/08) |
| Workflow | `FlowableApprovalEngine#startInstance` | Flowable only in the adapter; callers use `ApprovalProcesses` |
| Unit test | `TimesheetStateMachineTest#everyOperationInEveryStatus` | no Spring, fixed date |
| IT / acceptance | `AuditExportAndOwnRecordsIT#postgres`, `WorkCalendarMakeUpWeekendIT#postgres` | MockMvc + `TestPostgres.database`; `@DisplayName` starts with the card or rule id |

## B. Rules no test catches — follow them

1. Every query and list filters by data scope, fail-closed: empty scope = zero rows, unknown scope = deny, reading an id out of scope = 404 (`docs/ops/security-invariants.md` I-10).
2. Rounding happens once, in the money kernel; domain code never calls `setScale`, `divide` or picks a `RoundingMode`; weighted splits use `prorate`, conserving, largest remainder (CS-2.27, XD-08).
3. API money is `{amount: decimal string, currency: ISO 4217}` (XD-01); floats lose cents.
4. Aggregates that post or face the legal-entity × period gate carry `LegalEntityId` and `AccountingPeriod`; unknown source = fail. Config versions take effect on a period-aligned date, never retroactively (CS-7.5).
5. In a transaction: no outside calls, HTTP or batch work (use the outbox); take document numbers from `platform.numbering` `DocumentNumbers` here (CS-2.25).
6. Parse external input once at the web edge (`kernel.InvalidRequestException` → 400), then trust the types, no repeated null checks (CS-2.24). NullAway checks `@NullMarked` main code (not tests, which pass null on purpose), but not Jackson-filled request fields (validate at the edge) nor unannotated libraries such as JDBC `ResultSet` getters (taken as non-null: map nullable columns explicitly).
7. Missing or invalid input fails loudly (skip silently only if the rule card says so); no swallowed exceptions, no empty `catch` (CS-1.7, CS-2.24).
8. Events: past-tense immutable records of IDs and value objects, published in the same transaction after `save` (`DomainEvents`; CS-2.5). Listeners get redeliveries; make them idempotent (`ConsumedEvents`).
9. `JdbcClient` with named `:params` and explicit columns; never concatenate values into SQL; dynamic sort columns from an allow-list (CS-3.6, CS-3.7).
10. `RESTRICTED` data never in logs or exception messages (CS-6.2). Ask the user before creating accounts, starting containers (not local Testcontainers) or calling outside systems (CS-6.8).
11. Posted facts are never updated or deleted; corrections only by reversal (CS-3.9; guard G34 planned, the modules don't exist yet).
12. Files over 1000 lines must be split; aim for ≤ 400 per file and ≤ 60 per method (CS-1.15).
13. No single-caller pass-through wrappers.
14. Comment only a non-obvious why (constraint, rule id, quirk, trade-off, unit/zone, legacy evidence); a comment contradicting the code is a bug (CS-1.1).
15. Tests: red first (a bug's regression test fails on the base); deterministic waits, no broad `never()`; fixed `Clock` and `IdGenerator` (CS-5.6, CS-5.8, CS-7.3).

## C. Stack: old → new

`LegacyApiGuardTest` (LA-*) enforces the rows a check can catch; a failure names the replacement.

| Don't | Use |
|---|---|
| `javax.annotation`, `javax.inject` (not JDK `javax.sql`/`crypto`) | `jakarta.*` |
| `@MockBean`, `@SpyBean`; assuming `@SpringBootTest` brings MockMvc | `@MockitoBean`, `@MockitoSpyBean`; explicit `@AutoConfigureMockMvc` |
| `RestTemplate`, `TestRestTemplate` | `RestClient`, `RestTestClient` |
| `com.fasterxml.jackson.databind`, `ObjectMapper` | `tools.jackson`, `JsonMapper` (annotations unchanged) |
| `org.springframework.lang.Nullable` | `org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable` |
| `spring-retry` | spring-context `@Retryable`; never retry non-idempotent writes |
| JPA, Hibernate | `JdbcClient` |
| `spring-boot-starter-web` | `spring-boot-starter-webmvc` |
| `LocalDateTime`, `java.sql.Timestamp` | `Instant`, `LocalDate` |
| `Thread.sleep` in tests; Java preview features | deterministic waits; none |

## D. Guarded — a failing test names the fix

Modules talk only via `api`/`events` · layers: domain no Spring, web → application, JDBC in infrastructure · `@Transactional` only on use cases, one aggregate each · every endpoint `@PreAuthorize` or `@PublicEndpoint` · money only `kernel.Money`, no cross-currency math · audit and version rows append-only · merged migrations append-only (ADR-0038) · ASCII identifiers, terms from `docs/glossary/terms.yaml` · time only from injected `Clock`, stored as `timestamptz`, business date via `BusinessZone.today` · state changes only via aggregate methods · SQL only in `Jdbc*Repository`, own schema, registered · IDs are typed records, never ordered on · status enums, transitions in the aggregate · `POST` idempotency only for registered endpoints · `version` + `If-Match`/`ETag` · must-audit actions registered, no names in detail · `DomainException` + module `ErrorCode` · `Optional` returns, `@Nullable` response parts · events registered in asyncapi · controllers under `/api/v1/` · ITs on real PostgreSQL, own module only.

/**
 * #1171, the employee roster: {@code GET /api/v1/organization/employees?orgUnitId=&employmentStatus=&limit=&cursor=} and {@code .../employees/{employeeId}}
 * for the holders of {@code organization.workforce:employee:read}. The code is held by the system role personnel_specialist only, not by super_admin,
 * org_data_admin or any other (user 2026-11-03, intent 214823); the list is filtered by the caller's data scope, fail-closed; default lists employed
 * people only, {@code employmentStatus=ALL} or {@code SEPARATED} include the separated (same intent); a person with several assignments is in scope when
 * any assignment's department is in scope and the row lists every assignment; the detail lists all assignments with the reports-to person (user
 * 2026-11-04, intent 055504); an out-of-scope id answers 404 and writes the audit entry organization.workforce.employee.denied; no ID-card number,
 * ethnicity or household registration anywhere (ADR-0079:124, ADR-0080 U-14).
 *
 * <p>Contract fixed here, because the card names no field. A row: {@code employeeId, employeeCode, displayName, employmentStatus, hireDate,
 * assignments[]}; an assignment: {@code assignmentKind (PRIMARY|CONCURRENT), orgUnitId, orgUnitName, positionId, positionName}; the detail adds
 * {@code reportsToEmployeeId, reportsToDisplayName} to each assignment (null on a concurrent one). A page is {@code items} and {@code nextCursor}. A scope
 * that cannot be worked out answers 403 on the list and 404 on the detail (one-design, #730 comment 6597359528, as DataScopeReadBehaviourIT). The
 * roster is read from the versions (employee versions for status and hire date, assignment versions for unit, position and reporting line; the
 * concurrent assignment exists only there). The snapshot rows are written too, only because sign-in and scope resolution need them; both agree on unit and position.
 */
class EmployeeRosterHttpIT extends DataScopeSupport {

    static final TestDatabase postgres = TestPostgres.database(EmployeeRosterHttpIT.class, TestPostgres.MIGRATED).withUrlParam("currentSchema", "platform_approval");

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void database(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        postgres.register(registry);
    }

    static final String DENIED = "organization.workforce.employee.denied";
    static final LocalDate STARTED = LocalDate.of(2025, 3, 1);
    static final LocalDate STARTED_SUB = LocalDate.of(2024, 7, 15);
    static final LocalDate SINCE = LocalDate.of(2026, 1, 1);
    static final List<String> WORLD = List.of("a", "sub", "a1", "b", "c", "multi", "gone");
    static final Set<String> ROW_KEYS = Set.of("employeeId", "employeeCode", "displayName", "employmentStatus", "hireDate", "assignments");
    static final Set<String> ASSIGNMENT_KEYS = Set.of("assignmentKind", "orgUnitId", "orgUnitName", "positionId", "positionName", "reportsToEmployeeId", "reportsToDisplayName");
    static final Pattern FORBIDDEN_KEY = Pattern.compile("(?i)id.?card|idnumber|identity.?(card|number)|ethnic|household|national.?id");

    private final Map<String, String> unitIds = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    @MockitoSpyBean
    OrgDirectory organisation;

    @BeforeAll
    void theWorld() throws Exception {
        unit("RS-A", null);
        unit("RS-A1", "RS-A");
        unit("RS-B", null);
        unit("RS-C", null);
        unit("RS-X", null);
        position("RS-P1");
        position("RS-P2");
        for (String key : List.of("a", "sub", "gone", "ownReader")) {
            person(key, "RS-A", "RS-P1");
        }
        person("a1", "RS-A1", "RS-P1");
        person("b", "RS-B", "RS-P1");
        person("c", "RS-C", "RS-P2");
        person("multi", "RS-C", "RS-P2");
        for (String key : List.of("admin1", "admin2", "hr", "oda", "allReader", "subtreeReader", "bReader", "emptyReader", "brokenReader", "orgOnly", "cReader")) {
            person(key, "RS-X", "RS-P1");
        }
        seedOrganisation();
        administrator("admin1");
        administrator("admin2");
        grantByOperations("oda", ORG_DATA_ADMIN);
        grantByOperations("hr", "personnel_specialist");
        for (String code : List.of("RS-A", "RS-A1", "RS-B", "RS-C", "RS-X")) {
            unitIds.put(code, orgUnitId(session("oda"), code));
        }
        String[] perms = {EMPLOYEE_READ};
        roleWith("rs-all", "ALL", null, perms);
        roleWith("rs-subtree-a", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(withDescendants(unitIds.get("RS-A"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-only-b", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(unitIds.get("RS-B"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-only-c", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(unitIds.get("RS-C"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-nothing", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(UUID.randomUUID().toString())), perms);
        roleWith("rs-own", "OWN_ORG_UNIT", null, perms);
        roleWith("rs-org-unit-only", "ALL", null, ORG_UNIT_READ);
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "allReader", "rs-all");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "subtreeReader", "rs-subtree-a");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "bReader", "rs-only-b");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "cReader", "rs-only-c");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "emptyReader", "rs-nothing");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "ownReader", "rs-own");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "brokenReader", "rs-own");
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "orgOnly", "rs-org-unit-only");
        try (Connection workforce = schemaConnection("organization_workforce")) {
            for (String key : List.of("a", "gone", "a1", "b", "c", "multi")) {
                versions(workforce, key, unitOf(key), null, key.equals("gone") ? null : STARTED);
            }
            versions(workforce, "sub", "RS-A", "a", STARTED_SUB);
            UUID multi = UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf("multi"));
            UUID concurrent = AssignmentRoots.create(workforce, stored(multi), AssignmentRoots.CONCURRENT);
            assignmentVersion(workforce, multi, concurrent, AssignmentRoots.CONCURRENT, "RS-B", "RS-P2", null);
            execute(workforce, "update assignments set reports_to_employee_id = ? where employee_id = ?", stored(UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf("a"))), stored(UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf("sub"))));
            execute(workforce, "update employees set employment_status = 'SEPARATED', separated_at = now() where id = ?", stored(UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf("gone"))));
        }
    }

    private static String unitOf(String key) {
        return switch (key) {
            case "a", "gone" -> "RS-A";
            case "a1" -> "RS-A1";
            case "b" -> "RS-B";
            default -> "RS-C";
        };
    }

    /** The employee version and the primary assignment version of the person, both open from the same day; the separated person has no employment start. */
    private void versions(Connection workforce, String key, String unitCode, String reportsToKey, LocalDate started) throws Exception {
        UUID employee = UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(key));
        boolean employed = started != null;
        execute(workforce, """
                insert into employee_versions (employee_version_id, employee_id, effective_from_date, revision_number, employment_status, employment_phase_kind,
                    is_on_unpaid_leave, is_fee_earner, employment_start_date, employment_start_basis_kind, change_channel_kind, created_at, updated_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, false, false, ?, ?, 'MIGRATION', now(), now())""",
                Uuids.timeOrdered(NOW), stored(employee), employed ? started : SINCE, employed ? "EMPLOYED" : "SEPARATED", employed ? "REGULAR" : null, started,
                employed ? "DECLARED" : null);
        UUID root = AssignmentRoots.primaryOf(workforce, stored(employee));
        assignmentVersion(workforce, employee, root, AssignmentRoots.PRIMARY, unitCode, key.equals("c") || key.equals("multi") ? "RS-P2" : "RS-P1", reportsToKey == null ? null : UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(reportsToKey)));
    }

    private void assignmentVersion(Connection workforce, UUID employee, UUID root, String kind, String unitCode, String positionCode, UUID reportsTo) throws Exception {
        UUID position;
        try (PreparedStatement found = workforce.prepareStatement("select id from positions where code = ?")) {
            found.setString(1, positionCode);
            try (ResultSet rows = found.executeQuery()) {
                rows.next();
                position = rows.getObject(1, UUID.class);
            }
        }
        execute(workforce, """
                insert into assignment_versions (assignment_version_id, employee_id, assignment_id, assignment_kind, effective_from_date, revision_number,
                    org_unit_id, position_id, reports_to_employee_id, change_channel_kind, created_at, updated_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'MIGRATION', now(), now())""",
                Uuids.timeOrdered(NOW), stored(employee), root, kind, SINCE, kind.equals(AssignmentRoots.PRIMARY) ? 0 : 1, UUID.fromString(unitIds.get(unitCode)), position, reportsTo == null ? null : stored(reportsTo));
    }

    private static void execute(Connection connection, String sql, Object... args) throws Exception {
        try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                statement.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
            }
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @AfterEach
    void organisationWorksAgain() {
        Mockito.reset(organisation);
    }

    private void roleWith(String code, String scope, List<CustomOrgUnit> units, String... permissions) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse created = createRole(administrator("admin1"), roleBody(code, scope, units, permissions));
        if (created.getStatus() == 202) {
            String request = JsonPath.read(created.getContentAsString(), "$.requestId");
            require(confirm(administrator("admin2"), request), 204, "confirming role " + code);
            await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(roleIdOf(administrator("admin1"), code)).isPresent());
        } else {
            require(created, 201, "creating role " + code);
        }
    }

    // ---- calls ----

    private MockHttpServletResponse read(String key, String url) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get(url).with(as(session(key)))).andReturn().getResponse();
    }

    private List<Map<String, Object>> itemsOf(MockHttpServletResponse answer) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.items");
    }

    /** Every row of the list as the caller gets it, one row per page. */
    private List<Map<String, Object>> all(String key, String query) throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        String cursor = null;
        for (int page = 0; page < 60; page++) {
            MockHttpServletResponse answer = read(key, EMPLOYEES + "?" + query + (query.isEmpty() ? "" : "&") + "limit=1" + (cursor == null ? "" : "&cursor=" + cursor));
            require(answer, 200, key + " lists the employees");
            assertThat(itemsOf(answer)).hasSizeLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
            rows.addAll(itemsOf(answer));
            cursor = JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.nextCursor");
            if (cursor == null) {
                return rows;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("more than 60 pages of one row");
    }

    /** The keys of the people of the world among the rows (the callers are people of the organisation too and are left out). */
    private static Set<String> worldKeys(List<Map<String, Object>> rows) {
        Set<String> keys = new TreeSet<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
            String code = String.valueOf(row.get("employeeCode"));
            for (String key : WORLD) {
                if (code.equals(employeeCode(key))) {
                    keys.add(key);
                }
            }
        }
        return keys;
    }

    private static void collectKeys(Object node, Set<String> keys) {
        if (node instanceof Map<?, ?> map) {
            map.forEach((key, value) -> {
                keys.add(String.valueOf(key));
                collectKeys(value, keys);
            });
        } else if (node instanceof List<?> list) {
            list.forEach(item -> collectKeys(item, keys));
        }
    }

    // ---- the list ----

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（数据范围，A1-A3 风格）：本部门范围只看到本部门的人，含子部门范围看到部门及其下属，只含 B 的范围只看到 B（含在 B 有兼职的人），全公司范围看到所有在职的人，名单为空的范围一行也没有")
    void theListFollowsTheCallersDataScope() throws Exception {
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("ownReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "sub");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("subtreeReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "a1", "sub");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("bReader", ""))).containsExactly("b", "multi");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "a1", "b", "c", "multi", "sub");
        assertThat(all("emptyReader", "")).as("a scope that names no unit gives zero rows, not all rows").isEmpty();
        assertThat(all("emptyReader", "employmentStatus=ALL")).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（默认只列在职）：不带 employmentStatus 只有在职的人；ALL 含离职的人；SEPARATED 只有离职的人；离职的人同样受数据范围约束；不认识的取值是 400")
    void theDefaultListsEmployedPeopleOnly() throws Exception {
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", ""))).doesNotContain("gone");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "employmentStatus=ALL"))).contains("gone", "a", "b");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED"))).containsExactly("gone");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("ownReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED"))).as("the separated person lives in A").containsExactly("gone");
        assertThat(all("bReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED")).isEmpty();
        assertThat(read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "?employmentStatus=RETIRED").getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);
        Map<String, Object> gone = all("allReader", "employmentStatus=ALL").stream().filter(row -> employeeCode("gone").equals(row.get("employeeCode"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(gone.get("employmentStatus")).isEqualTo("SEPARATED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（多任职）：一个人的主任职在 C、兼职在 B：只管 B 的人看得到他，只管 C 的范围也看得到他，管不到 B 也管不到 C 的人看不到；这一行列出他的每一个任职（主任职与兼职，各带部门与岗位）")
    void aPersonWithSeveralAssignmentsIsInScopeThroughAny() throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> multi = all("bReader", "").stream().filter(row -> employeeCode("multi").equals(row.get("employeeCode"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();

        List<Map<String, Object>> assignments = JsonPath.parse(multi).read("$.assignments");
        assertThat(assignments).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("assignmentKind")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("PRIMARY", "CONCURRENT");
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("orgUnitId")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(unitIds.get("RS-C"), unitIds.get("RS-B"));
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("orgUnitName")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(unitName("RS-C"), unitName("RS-B"));
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("positionName")).containsOnly(positionName("RS-P2"));
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("cReader", ""))).containsExactly("c", "multi");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("subtreeReader", ""))).doesNotContain("multi");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（分页与按部门筛选）：limit=1 逐页取遍与一次取全相同；orgUnitId 只留下该部门里有任职的人；每页不超过 limit")
    void pagingAndTheUnitFilter() throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse whole = read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "?limit=200");
        require(whole, 200, "allReader reads one big page");
        assertThat(worldKeys(itemsOf(whole))).isEqualTo(worldKeys(all("allReader", "")));
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "orgUnitId=" + unitIds.get("RS-B")))).containsExactly("b", "multi");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "orgUnitId=" + unitIds.get("RS-A1")))).containsExactly("a1");
        assertThat(all("bReader", "orgUnitId=" + unitIds.get("RS-A"))).as("the filter cannot widen the data scope").isEmpty();
        assertThat(all("subtreeReader", "orgUnitId=" + unitIds.get("RS-C"))).as("the filter cannot widen the data scope").isEmpty();
    }

    // ---- the detail ----

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 2 条（详情）：姓名、工号、状态、入职日期、每个任职的部门与岗位；有直属领导的人给出领导的员工 ID 和姓名，兼职任职没有汇报线；兼职的人详情里有两个任职")
    void theDetailShowsTheRosterAndTheReportsToPerson() throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse sub = read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("sub"));
        require(sub, 200, "allReader reads one employee");
        Map<String, Object> body = JsonPath.parse(sub.getContentAsString()).read("$");
        assertThat(body).containsEntry("employeeId", employeeIdOf("sub")).containsEntry("employeeCode", employeeCode("sub")).containsEntry("displayName", "Synthetic sub")
                .containsEntry("employmentStatus", "EMPLOYED").containsEntry("hireDate", STARTED_SUB.toString());
        List<Map<String, Object>> subAssignments = JsonPath.parse(sub.getContentAsString()).read("$.assignments");
        assertThat(subAssignments).singleElement().satisfies(item -> {
            assertThat(item.get("assignmentKind")).isEqualTo("PRIMARY");
            assertThat(item.get("orgUnitId")).isEqualTo(unitIds.get("RS-A"));
            assertThat(item.get("reportsToEmployeeId")).isEqualTo(employeeIdOf("a"));
            assertThat(item.get("reportsToDisplayName")).isEqualTo("Synthetic a");
        });
        MockHttpServletResponse multi = read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("multi"));
        require(multi, 200, "allReader reads the person with two assignments");
        List<Map<String, Object>> multiAssignments = JsonPath.parse(multi.getContentAsString()).read("$.assignments");
        assertThat(multiAssignments).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(multiAssignments).filteredOn(item -> "CONCURRENT".equals(item.get("assignmentKind"))).singleElement().satisfies(item -> {
            assertThat(item.get("orgUnitId")).isEqualTo(unitIds.get("RS-B"));
            assertThat(item.get("reportsToEmployeeId")).isNull();
        });
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 2 条（范围外的详情）：范围外的员工 ID 是 404（错误码以 _NOT_FOUND 结尾），回答与根本不存在的 ID 一样；每次被拒的读写一条审计 organization.workforce.employee.denied，审计里没有工号")
    void aDetailOutOfScopeIsNotFoundAndAudited() throws Exception {
        int before = auditedActions(DENIED).size();
        String absent = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

        MockHttpServletResponse refused = read("bReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("a"));
        MockHttpServletResponse nothing = read("bReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + absent);

        assertThat(refused.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
        assertThat(nothing.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
        assertThat(String.valueOf(JsonPath.<String>read(refused.getContentAsString(), "$.errorCode"))).endsWith("_NOT_FOUND");
        assertThat(JsonPath.<String>read(refused.getContentAsString(), "$.errorCode")).isEqualTo(JsonPath.<String>read(nothing.getContentAsString(), "$.errorCode"));
        assertThat(refused.getContentAsString().replace(employeeIdOf("a"), "<id>")).doesNotContain(employeeCode("a"));
        read("bReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("c"));
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(auditedActions(DENIED)).as("one denied entry per out-of-scope read of an existing employee").hasSize(before + 2));
        assertThat(read("bReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("b")).getStatus()).as("an employee in scope is readable").isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(auditedActions(DENIED)).hasSize(before + 2);
        assertThat(auditEntries(DENIED).stream().map(DataScopeSupport::auditText).collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.joining(" "))).as("no employee code in the audit text")
                .doesNotContain(employeeCode("a"), employeeCode("c"));
    }

    // ---- what is never shown, who may read ----

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 3 条（不出现的字段）：名册行与详情只有约定的键；任何层级的键都不像身份证号、民族、户籍；返回内容里没有邮箱、登录名")
    void noIdentityCardEthnicityOrHouseholdAnywhere() throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse list = read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "?limit=200&employmentStatus=ALL");
        MockHttpServletResponse detail = read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("sub"));
        require(list, 200, "the roster");
        require(detail, 200, "the detail");

        for (Map<String, Object> row : itemsOf(list)) {
            assertThat(row.keySet()).as("a roster row carries only the agreed keys").isSubsetOf(ROW_KEYS).contains("employeeId", "employeeCode", "displayName", "employmentStatus", "assignments");
        }
        Set<String> keys = new TreeSet<>();
        collectKeys(JsonPath.parse(list.getContentAsString()).read("$"), keys);
        collectKeys(JsonPath.parse(detail.getContentAsString()).read("$"), keys);
        assertThat(keys).noneMatch(key -> FORBIDDEN_KEY.matcher(key).find());
        Map<String, Object> detailBody = JsonPath.parse(detail.getContentAsString()).read("$");
        assertThat(detailBody.keySet()).isSubsetOf(ROW_KEYS);
        List<Map<String, Object>> assignments = JsonPath.parse(detail.getContentAsString()).read("$.assignments");
        assertThat(assignments).allSatisfy(item -> assertThat(item.keySet()).isSubsetOf(ASSIGNMENT_KEYS));
        assertThat(list.getContentAsString()).doesNotContain("@example.test").doesNotContain("f1-login-");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 6、7 条（谁能读）：持有 employee:read 的角色可读；人事专员（系统角色）可读；超级管理员、组织数据管理员、只有 org-unit:read 的人在名册列表和详情上都是 403；未登录是 401")
    void onlyTheHolderOfTheRosterPermissionMayRead() throws Exception {
        String url = EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("a");
        assertThat(read("hr", EMPLOYEES).getStatus()).as("personnel_specialist holds the code").isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(read("hr", url).getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("hr", ""))).containsExactly("a", "a1", "b", "c", "multi", "sub");
        for (String key : List.of("oda", "orgOnly")) {
            assertThat(read(key, EMPLOYEES).getStatus()).as(key + " lists").isEqualTo(403);
            assertThat(read(key, url).getStatus()).as(key + " reads one").isEqualTo(403);
        }
        MockHttpServletResponse superList = mvc.perform(get(EMPLOYEES).with(as(administrator("admin1")))).andReturn().getResponse();
        MockHttpServletResponse superDetail = mvc.perform(get(url).with(as(administrator("admin1")))).andReturn().getResponse();
        assertThat(superList.getStatus()).as("super_admin does not hold the roster code: " + superList.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(superDetail.getStatus()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(mvc.perform(get(EMPLOYEES)).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);
        assertThat(mvc.perform(get(url)).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（范围算不出来）：本部门范围的人其所在单位查不出来时，列表是 403、详情是 404，绝不给出更宽的回答")
    void anUnresolvableScopeIsDenied() throws Exception {
        String employee = employeeIdOf("brokenReader");
        require(read("brokenReader", EMPLOYEES), 200, "priming the principal of the caller before the organisation fails");
        doThrow(new IllegalStateException("organisation unavailable")).when(organisation).findEmployee(argThat(id -> id != null && id.value().toString().equals(employee)));

        assertThat(read("brokenReader", EMPLOYEES).getStatus()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(read("brokenReader", EMPLOYEES + "?employmentStatus=ALL").getStatus()).isEqualTo(403);
        assertThat(read("brokenReader", EMPLOYEES + "/" + employeeIdOf("a")).getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.acceptance.organization.workforce;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;

import com.forcitis.one.TestDatabase;
import com.forcitis.one.TestPostgres;
import com.forcitis.one.acceptance.DataScopeSupport;
import com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockHttpServletResponse;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertyRegistry;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertySource;

/**
 * #1171, the position directory: {@code GET /api/v1/organization/positions?status=&limit=&cursor=} and {@code GET .../positions/{positionId}} give every
 * position's code, name, status and the dates of its current version, nothing else (ADR-0068 item 4: no headcount, no duties), to every logged-in
 * user (user 2026-11-04, intent 055504); an anonymous call answers 401. The register line is NOT_SCOPE_BOUND, company-level reference data.
 *
 * <p>Contract fixed here, because the card names no field: a row is {@code positionId, code, name, status, effectiveFromDate, effectiveToDate}
 * (the last one absent or null while the version is open); a page is {@code items} and {@code nextCursor} (null on the last page) as the other
 * lists of the module answer. The directory is read from the position versions, not from the snapshot: the current version (not superseded, in force
 * today) decides code, name and status.
 */
class PositionDirectoryHttpIT extends DataScopeSupport {

    static final TestDatabase postgres = TestPostgres.database(PositionDirectoryHttpIT.class, TestPostgres.MIGRATED).withUrlParam("currentSchema", "platform_approval");

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void database(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        postgres.register(registry);
    }

    static final String POSITIONS = "/api/v1/organization/positions";
    static final LocalDate SINCE = LocalDate.of(2026, 1, 1);
    static final LocalDate RENAMED_ON = LocalDate.of(2026, 6, 1);
    static final Set<String> ROW_KEYS = Set.of("positionId", "code", "name", "status", "effectiveFromDate", "effectiveToDate");

    private final Map<String, UUID> positionIds = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();

    @BeforeAll
    void theWorld() throws Exception {
        unit("PD-U", null);
        position("PD-SEED");
        for (String key : List.of("admin1", "admin2", "reader", "plain")) {
            person(key, "PD-U", "PD-SEED");
        }
        seedOrganisation();
        administrator("admin1");
        administrator("admin2");
        ordinaryRole(administrator("admin1"), "pd-org-unit-reader", "ALL", null, ORG_UNIT_READ);
        grantOrdinaryRoles(administrator("admin1"), "reader", "pd-org-unit-reader");
        try (Connection workforce = schemaConnection("organization_workforce")) {
            version(workforce, "active", "PD-ACTIVE", "Synthetic active post", "ACTIVE", SINCE, null);
            version(workforce, "disabled", "PD-DISABLED", "Synthetic disabled post", "DISABLED", SINCE, null);
            version(workforce, "renamed", "PD-RENAMED", "Synthetic old name", "ACTIVE", SINCE, RENAMED_ON);
            version(workforce, "renamed", "PD-RENAMED", "Synthetic new name", "ACTIVE", RENAMED_ON, null);
        }
    }

    /** One position version written directly (the MIGRATION channel); the position row itself on first use. */
    private void version(Connection workforce, String key, String code, String name, String status, LocalDate from, LocalDate to) throws Exception {
        boolean first = !positionIds.containsKey(key);
        UUID id = positionIds.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> UUID.randomUUID());
        if (first) {
            run(workforce, "insert into positions (id, code, name, sort_order, status) values (?, ?, ?, 1, ?)", id, code, name, status);
        }
        run(workforce, """
                insert into position_versions (position_version_id, position_id, effective_from_date, effective_to_date, revision_number, code, name, sort_order, status,
                    change_channel_kind, closed_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, 1, ?, 'MIGRATION', case when ?::date is null then null else now() end)""",
                UUID.randomUUID(), id, from, to, code, name, status, to);
    }

    private static void run(Connection connection, String sql, Object... args) throws Exception {
        try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                statement.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
            }
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    private MockHttpServletResponse read(String key, String url) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get(url).with(as(session(key)))).andReturn().getResponse();
    }

    private List<Map<String, Object>> itemsOf(MockHttpServletResponse answer) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.items");
    }

    /** Every row of the list, one row per page. */
    private List<Map<String, Object>> all(String key, String query) throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        String cursor = null;
        for (int page = 0; page < 30; page++) {
            MockHttpServletResponse answer = read(key, POSITIONS + "?" + query + (query.isEmpty() ? "" : "&") + "limit=1" + (cursor == null ? "" : "&cursor=" + cursor));
            require(answer, 200, key + " lists the positions");
            assertThat(itemsOf(answer)).hasSizeLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
            rows.addAll(itemsOf(answer));
            cursor = JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.nextCursor");
            if (cursor == null) {
                return rows;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("more than 30 pages of one row");
    }

    private static Set<String> codes(List<Map<String, Object>> rows) {
        return rows.stream().map(row -> String.valueOf(row.get("code"))).collect(Collectors.toSet());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 4 条（目录内容）：目录给出每个岗位的编码、名称、状态和当前版本的起止日，已被改名的岗位只给当前名称；行里只有约定的键（无编制人数、无职责）")
    void theDirectoryShowsTheCurrentVersionOfEveryPosition() throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = all("reader", "");

        assertThat(codes(rows)).contains("PD-ACTIVE", "PD-DISABLED", "PD-RENAMED");
        Map<String, Object> renamed = rows.stream().filter(row -> "PD-RENAMED".equals(row.get("code"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(rows.stream().filter(row -> "PD-RENAMED".equals(row.get("code"))).count()).as("one row per position, not per version").isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(renamed.get("name")).isEqualTo("Synthetic new name");
        assertThat(renamed.get("status")).isEqualTo("ACTIVE");
        assertThat(renamed.get("positionId")).isEqualTo(positionIds.get("renamed").toString());
        assertThat(renamed.get("effectiveFromDate")).isEqualTo(RENAMED_ON.toString());
        assertThat(renamed.get("effectiveToDate")).isNull();
        Map<String, Object> disabled = rows.stream().filter(row -> "PD-DISABLED".equals(row.get("code"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(disabled.get("status")).isEqualTo("DISABLED");
        for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
            assertThat(row.keySet()).as("a position row carries only the agreed keys").isSubsetOf(ROW_KEYS).contains("positionId", "code", "name", "status", "effectiveFromDate");
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 4 条（状态过滤与分页）：status=ACTIVE 不含停用岗位，status=DISABLED 只含停用岗位；limit=1 逐页取遍的结果与不分页相同；不认识的 status 是 400")
    void theStatusFilterAndThePaging() throws Exception {
        Set<String> everything = codes(all("reader", ""));
        assertThat(codes(all("reader", "status=ACTIVE"))).contains("PD-ACTIVE", "PD-RENAMED").doesNotContain("PD-DISABLED");
        assertThat(codes(all("reader", "status=DISABLED"))).contains("PD-DISABLED").doesNotContain("PD-ACTIVE", "PD-RENAMED");
        MockHttpServletResponse whole = read("reader", POSITIONS + "?limit=200");
        require(whole, 200, "reader lists the positions in one page");
        assertThat(codes(itemsOf(whole))).isEqualTo(everything);
        assertThat(read("reader", POSITIONS + "?status=NOT_A_STATUS").getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 4 条（单个岗位）：按 positionId 取得与列表同一行；不存在的 id 是 404 且错误码以 _NOT_FOUND 结尾")
    void onePositionAndAnUnknownId() throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse found = read("reader", POSITIONS + "/" + positionIds.get("renamed"));

        require(found, 200, "reader reads one position");
        Map<String, Object> body = JsonPath.parse(found.getContentAsString()).read("$");
        assertThat(body).containsEntry("positionId", positionIds.get("renamed").toString()).containsEntry("code", "PD-RENAMED").containsEntry("name", "Synthetic new name");
        assertThat(body.keySet()).isSubsetOf(ROW_KEYS);
        MockHttpServletResponse unknown = read("reader", POSITIONS + "/" + UUID.randomUUID());
        assertThat(unknown.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
        assertThat(String.valueOf(JsonPath.<String>read(unknown.getContentAsString(), "$.errorCode"))).endsWith("_NOT_FOUND");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 6 条（任何登录用户都能看）：只有员工角色的人、只有 org-unit:read 的人都得到 200，目录内容相同；未登录得到 401（列表与单个都是）")
    void everyLoggedInUserReadsTheDirectoryAndAnAnonymousCallIsRefused() throws Exception {
        Set<String> expected = codes(all("reader", ""));

        assertThat(codes(all("plain", ""))).isEqualTo(expected);
        assertThat(read("plain", POSITIONS + "/" + positionIds.get("active")).getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(mvc.perform(get(POSITIONS)).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);
        assertThat(mvc.perform(get(POSITIONS + "/" + positionIds.get("active"))).andReturn().getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);
    }

    private static String unitOf(String key) {
        return switch (key) {
            case "a", "gone" -> "RS-A";
            case "a1" -> "RS-A1";
            case "b" -> "RS-B";
            default -> "RS-C";
        };
    }

    /** The employee version and the primary assignment version of the person, both open from the same day; the separated person has no employment start. */
    private void versions(Connection workforce, String key, String unitCode, String reportsToKey, LocalDate started) throws Exception {
        UUID employee = UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(key));
        boolean employed = started != null;
        execute(workforce, """
                insert into employee_versions (employee_version_id, employee_id, effective_from_date, revision_number, employment_status, employment_phase_kind,
                    is_on_unpaid_leave, is_fee_earner, employment_start_date, employment_start_basis_kind, change_channel_kind, created_at, updated_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, false, false, ?, ?, 'MIGRATION', now(), now())""",
                Uuids.timeOrdered(NOW), stored(employee), employed ? started : SINCE, employed ? "EMPLOYED" : "SEPARATED", employed ? "REGULAR" : null, started,
                employed ? "DECLARED" : null);
        UUID root = AssignmentRoots.primaryOf(workforce, stored(employee));
        assignmentVersion(workforce, employee, root, AssignmentRoots.PRIMARY, unitCode, key.equals("c") || key.equals("multi") ? "RS-P2" : "RS-P1", reportsToKey == null ? null : UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(reportsToKey)));
    }

    private void assignmentVersion(Connection workforce, UUID employee, UUID root, String kind, String unitCode, String positionCode, UUID reportsTo) throws Exception {
        UUID position;
        try (PreparedStatement found = workforce.prepareStatement("select id from positions where code = ?")) {
            found.setString(1, positionCode);
            try (ResultSet rows = found.executeQuery()) {
                rows.next();
                position = rows.getObject(1, UUID.class);
            }
        }
        execute(workforce, """
                insert into assignment_versions (assignment_version_id, employee_id, assignment_id, assignment_kind, effective_from_date, revision_number,
                    org_unit_id, position_id, reports_to_employee_id, change_channel_kind, created_at, updated_at)
                values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'MIGRATION', now(), now())""",
                Uuids.timeOrdered(NOW), stored(employee), root, kind, SINCE, kind.equals(AssignmentRoots.PRIMARY) ? 0 : 1, UUID.fromString(unitIds.get(unitCode)), position, reportsTo == null ? null : stored(reportsTo));
    }

    private static void execute(Connection connection, String sql, Object... args) throws Exception {
        try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                statement.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
            }
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @AfterEach
    void organisationWorksAgain() {
        Mockito.reset(organisation);
    }

    private void roleWith(String code, String scope, List<CustomOrgUnit> units, String... permissions) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse created = createRole(administrator("admin1"), roleBody(code, scope, units, permissions));
        if (created.getStatus() == 202) {
            String request = JsonPath.read(created.getContentAsString(), "$.requestId");
            require(confirm(administrator("admin2"), request), 204, "confirming role " + code);
            await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(roleIdOf(administrator("admin1"), code)).isPresent());
        } else {
            require(created, 201, "creating role " + code);
        }
    }

    // ---- calls ----

    private MockHttpServletResponse read(String key, String url) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get(url).with(as(session(key)))).andReturn().getResponse();
    }

    private List<Map<String, Object>> itemsOf(MockHttpServletResponse answer) throws Exception {
        return JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.items");
    }

    /** Every row of the list as the caller gets it, one row per page. */
    private List<Map<String, Object>> all(String key, String query) throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        String cursor = null;
        for (int page = 0; page < 60; page++) {
            MockHttpServletResponse answer = read(key, EMPLOYEES + "?" + query + (query.isEmpty() ? "" : "&") + "limit=1" + (cursor == null ? "" : "&cursor=" + cursor));
            require(answer, 200, key + " lists the employees");
            assertThat(itemsOf(answer)).hasSizeLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
            rows.addAll(itemsOf(answer));
            cursor = JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.nextCursor");
            if (cursor == null) {
                return rows;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("more than 60 pages of one row");
    }

    /** The keys of the people of the world among the rows (the callers are people of the organisation too and are left out). */
    private static Set<String> worldKeys(List<Map<String, Object>> rows) {
        Set<String> keys = new TreeSet<>();
        for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
            String code = String.valueOf(row.get("employeeCode"));
            for (String key : WORLD) {
                if (code.equals(employeeCode(key))) {
                    keys.add(key);
                }
            }
        }
        return keys;
    }

    private static void collectKeys(Object node, Set<String> keys) {
        if (node instanceof Map<?, ?> map) {
            map.forEach((key, value) -> {
                keys.add(String.valueOf(key));
                collectKeys(value, keys);
            });
        } else if (node instanceof List<?> list) {
            list.forEach(item -> collectKeys(item, keys));
        }
    }

    // ---- the list ----

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（数据范围，A1-A3 风格）：本部门范围只看到本部门的人，含子部门范围看到部门及其下属，只含 B 的范围只看到 B（含在 B 有兼职的人），全公司范围看到所有在职的人，名单为空的范围一行也没有")
    void theListFollowsTheCallersDataScope() throws Exception {
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("ownReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "sub");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("subtreeReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "a1", "sub");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("bReader", ""))).containsExactly("b", "multi");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", ""))).containsExactly("a", "a1", "b", "c", "multi", "sub");
        assertThat(all("emptyReader", "")).as("a scope that names no unit gives zero rows, not all rows").isEmpty();
        assertThat(all("emptyReader", "employmentStatus=ALL")).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#1171 完成标准第 1 条（默认只列在职）：不带 employmentStatus 只有在职的人；ALL 含离职的人；SEPARATED 只有离职的人；离职的人同样受数据范围约束；不认识的取值是 400")
    void theDefaultListsEmployedPeopleOnly() throws Exception {
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", ""))).doesNotContain("gone");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "employmentStatus=ALL"))).contains("gone", "a", "b");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("allReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED"))).containsExactly("gone");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("ownReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED"))).as("the separated person lives in A").containsExactly("gone");
        assertThat(all("bReader", "employmentStatus=SEPARATED")).isEmpty();
        assertThat(read("allReader", EMPLOYEES + "?employmentStatus=RETIRED").getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);
        Map<String, Object> gone = all("allReader", "employmentStatus=ALL").stream().filter(row -> employeeCode("gone").equals(row.get("employeeCode"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(gone.get("employmentStatus")).isEqualTo("SEPARATED");
    }

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubjectKind;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgHierarchy;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

/** A unit outside the caller's scope answers like one that does not exist, and the attempt is audited (ADR-0026 section 1 item 7). */
@Service
public class GetOrgUnit {
    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
    private final AuditLog audit;
    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
    private final CurrentUser currentUser;

    GetOrgUnit(OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots, AuditLog audit, OrgUnitAccess access, CurrentUser currentUser) {
        this.snapshots = snapshots;
        this.audit = audit;
        this.access = access;
        this.currentUser = currentUser;
    }

    public Optional<OrgUnitDetailView> execute(UUID orgUnitId) {
        OrgUnitId id = new OrgUnitId(orgUnitId);
        var all = snapshots.findAllOrgUnits();
        Map<OrgUnitId, OrgUnit> byId = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        all.forEach(u -> byId.put(u.orgUnitId(), u));
        OrgUnit unit = byId.get(id);
        if (unit == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        Set<OrgUnitId> allowed = OrgUnitVisibility.idsInForDetail(all, access);
        if (!allowed.contains(id)) {
            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.org-unit.denied", currentUser.userId(), null, AuditSubject.of(AuditSubjectKind.ORG_UNIT, orgUnitId),
                    "outside the scope of the caller"));
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return Optional.of(new OrgUnitDetailView(OrgUnitView.of(unit),
                OrgHierarchy.ancestors(byId, id).stream().filter(allowed::contains).map(OrgUnitId::value).toList(),
                OrgHierarchy.unitAndBelow(all, id).stream().filter(allowed::contains).map(OrgUnitId::value).toList()));
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgHierarchy;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnitStatus;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

/** Flat list, parents first, only the units the caller may see; the client builds the tree from parentOrgUnitId. */
@Service
public class ListOrgUnits {
    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
    private final OrgUnitAccess access;

    ListOrgUnits(OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots, OrgUnitAccess access) {
        this.snapshots = snapshots;
        this.access = access;
    }

    public List<OrgUnitView> execute(boolean includeRevoked) {
        List<OrgUnit> all = snapshots.findAllOrgUnits();
        Set<OrgUnitId> allowed = OrgUnitVisibility.idsIn(all, access.visibleOrgUnits(OrgUnitVisibility.ORG_UNIT_READ));
        return OrgHierarchy.parentsFirst(all.stream()
                .filter(unit -> allowed.contains(unit.orgUnitId()))
                .filter(unit -> includeRevoked || unit.status() != OrgUnitStatus.REVOKED)
                .toList()).stream()
                .map(OrgUnitView::of)
                .toList();
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgHierarchy;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

/** Turns what the caller may see into the ids of the units that exist: a unit outside it is not there for the caller. */
final class OrgUnitVisibility {
    /** The permission whose org unit scope decides what can be seen (a copy of platform.identity Permission.Codes, checked by WorkforceConfigurationTest). */
    static final String ORG_UNIT_READ = "organization.workforce:org-unit:read";

    private OrgUnitVisibility() {
    }

    /**
     * For a detail: a scope that cannot be worked out shows nothing, so the id answers like a missing one; a refusal here would tell the caller the id exists
     * (security-invariants I-10, #730 comment 6606893624). Lists keep the refusal, they carry no id.
     */
    static Set<OrgUnitId> idsInForDetail(Collection<OrgUnit> units, OrgUnitAccess access) {
        try {
            return idsIn(units, access.visibleOrgUnits(ORG_UNIT_READ));
        } catch (DomainException unavailable) {
            if (unavailable.kind() != ErrorKind.FORBIDDEN) {
                throw unavailable;
            }
            return Set.of();
        }
    }

    static Set<OrgUnitId> idsIn(Collection<OrgUnit> units, OrgUnitAccess.Visible visible) {
        Set<OrgUnitId> ids = new HashSet<>();
        if (visible.everything()) {
            units.forEach(unit -> ids.add(unit.orgUnitId()));
            return ids;
        }
        visible.orgUnits().forEach(id -> ids.add(new OrgUnitId(id)));
        visible.orgUnitSubtrees().forEach(root -> ids.addAll(OrgHierarchy.unitAndBelow(units, new OrgUnitId(root))));
        return ids;
    }
}

                          boolean deliveryUnit, String shortName) {
    static OrgUnitView of(OrgUnit unit) {

                unit.parentOrgUnitId() == null ? null : unit.parentOrgUnitId().value(), unit.sortOrder(),
                unit.status().name(), unit.headEmployeeId() == null ? null : unit.headEmployeeId().value(),
                unit.regionalReceivablesManagerId() == null ? null : unit.regionalReceivablesManagerId().value(),
                unit.regionCategory() == null ? null : unit.regionCategory().name(), unit.deliveryUnit(), unit.shortName());

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.web;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.WorkforceError;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.GetOrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.ListOrgUnits;
import java.util.List;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import java.util.UUID;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization/org-units")
class OrgUnitController {
    private final ListOrgUnits listOrgUnits;
    private final GetOrgUnit getOrgUnit;

    OrgUnitController(ListOrgUnits listOrgUnits, GetOrgUnit getOrgUnit) {
        this.listOrgUnits = listOrgUnits;
        this.getOrgUnit = getOrgUnit;
    }

    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_UNIT_READ + "')")
    List<OrgUnitResponse> list(@RequestParam(defaultValue = "false") boolean includeRevoked) {
        return listOrgUnits.execute(includeRevoked).stream().map(OrgUnitResponse::of).toList();
    }

    @GetMapping(path = "/{orgUnitId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_UNIT_READ + "')")
    OrgUnitDetailResponse get(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId) {
        return getOrgUnit.execute(orgUnitId)
                .map(OrgUnitDetailResponse::of).orElseThrow(() -> new DomainException(
                        WorkforceError.ORGANIZATION_WORKFORCE_ORG_UNIT_NOT_FOUND, ErrorKind.NOT_FOUND, "no org unit " + orgUnitId));
    }
}

package com.forcitis.one.kernel;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Which org units the signed-in person may see through a permission, worked out from the scope of the enabled roles that grant it.
 * Implemented by whoever owns roles, asked by whoever serves org units, so the org module needs no dependency on it. When the scope
 * cannot be worked out the call throws: nothing is shown instead of everything (ADR-0026, RULE-350).
 */
public interface OrgUnitAccess {

    Visible visibleOrgUnits(String permissionCode);

    /** orgUnits are visible as such, orgUnitSubtrees with everything below them; empty and not everything means nothing. */
    record Visible(boolean everything, Set<UUID> orgUnits, Set<UUID> orgUnitSubtrees) {
        public Visible {
            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
            orgUnitSubtrees = Set.copyOf(orgUnitSubtrees);
        }

        public static Visible all() {
            return new Visible(true, Set.of(), Set.of());
        }

        public static Visible nothing() {
            return new Visible(false, Set.of(), Set.of());
        }
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.kernel;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.charset.CharacterCodingException;
import java.nio.charset.CodingErrorAction;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * limit defaults to 50 and is at most 200 (ListLimits). The cursor is opaque to the caller, versioned, unsigned, and means one list only:
 * it only says where to go on, the data scope is enforced in the query, so a forged cursor reaches nothing a caller may not read.
 */
public record PageRequest(int limit, @Nullable String cursorValue) {
    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(PageRequest.class);
    private static final String CURSOR_VERSION = "v1";
    /** Room for the longest key of any list (128 characters, up to 3 bytes each) plus version and list name, as base64. */
    private static final int MAX_CURSOR_LENGTH = 1024;

    public PageRequest {
        if (limit < 1 || limit > ListLimits.MAX_LIMIT) {
            throw new InvalidRequestException("limit must be between 1 and " + ListLimits.MAX_LIMIT);
        }
    }

    public static PageRequest of(@Nullable Integer limit, @Nullable String cursor, String list) {
        return new PageRequest(limit == null ? ListLimits.DEFAULT_LIMIT : limit, decode(cursor, list).orElse(null));
    }

    public @Nullable UUID cursorUuid() {
        if (cursorValue == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return Uuids.parseCanonical(cursorValue);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException notUuid) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor key is not a UUID");
        }
    }

    public @Nullable Integer cursorNumber() {
        if (cursorValue == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return Integer.valueOf(cursorValue);
        } catch (NumberFormatException notNumber) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor key is not a whole number");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Cuts the page out of a list that is already in its final order; key names a row uniquely and is what the cursor carries.
     *
     * @deprecated lists page by key in the database; callers move over in K-3b and this is deleted with the last one.
     */
    @Deprecated
    public <T> CursorPage<T> slice(java.util.List<T> ordered, java.util.function.Function<T, String> key, String list) {
        int start = 0;
        if (cursorValue != null) {
            start = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < ordered.size(); i++) {
                if (key.apply(ordered.get(i)).equals(cursorValue)) {
                    start = i + 1;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (start < 0) {
                throw new InvalidCursorException("the row the cursor points at is gone");
            }
        }
        int end = Math.min(ordered.size(), start + limit);
        java.util.List<T> items = ordered.subList(start, end);
        return CursorPage.of(items, end < ordered.size() ? encode(list, key.apply(items.getLast())) : null);
    }

    public static String encode(String list, String key) {
        String cursor = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString((CURSOR_VERSION + ":" + list + ":" + key).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        try {
            decode(cursor, list);
        } catch (InvalidCursorException unreadable) {
            LOG.error("ALERT kernel the cursor of list {} cannot be issued: it would not read back ({})", list, unreadable.getMessage());
            throw new IllegalStateException("a cursor of list " + list + " would not read back: " + unreadable.getMessage());
        }
        return cursor;
    }

    private static Optional<String> decode(@Nullable String cursor, String list) {
        if (cursor == null || cursor.isBlank()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        String[] parts = readText(cursor).split(":", 3);
        if (parts.length != 3 || !CURSOR_VERSION.equals(parts[0])) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor is not one this server issued");
        }
        if (!parts[1].equals(list)) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor belongs to another list");
        }
        if (parts[2].isEmpty() || parts[2].chars().anyMatch(Character::isISOControl)) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor carries no usable key");
        }
        return Optional.of(parts[2]);
    }

    private static String readText(String cursor) {
        if (cursor.length() > MAX_CURSOR_LENGTH) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor is not one this server issued");
        }
        byte[] bytes;
        try {
            bytes = Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(cursor);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException malformed) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor is not one this server issued");
        }
        if (!Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(bytes).equals(cursor)) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor is not one this server issued");
        }
        try {
            return StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder().onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT).onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
                    .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString();
        } catch (CharacterCodingException malformed) {
            throw new InvalidCursorException("the cursor is not one this server issued");
        }
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.kernel;

import java.util.List;
import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;

/** One page of a list: nextCursor is null on the last page. Every list endpoint answers this shape. */
public record CursorPage<T>(List<T> items, @Nullable String nextCursor) {
    public CursorPage {
        items = List.copyOf(items);
    }

    public static <T> CursorPage<T> of(List<T> items, @Nullable String nextCursor) {
        return new CursorPage<>(items, nextCursor);
    }
}

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;

/**
 * Reads the current (non-superseded) employee versions by business day: the unpaid-leave flag (ADR-0068 section 3) and the employment status. An
 * employee without any version is read from the snapshot until the initial migration (#960).

public interface EmployeeVersionQueries {

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.WecomUserId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmployeeLabel;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.StaffEmployee;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
class JdbcOrgSnapshotQueries implements OrgSnapshotQueries {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final BusinessZone zone;

    JdbcOrgSnapshotQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.zone = zone;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StaffEmployee> findStaff(EmployeeId employeeId) {
        return jdbc.sql(OrgSnapshotRows.SELECT_STAFF_ON_DAY + """
                 and s.id = :id
                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", employeeId.value()).query((rs, n) -> OrgSnapshotRows.staff(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean employeeExists(EmployeeId employeeId) {
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(jdbc.sql("""
                select exists(select 1 from organization_workforce.employees where id = :id)
                """)
                .param("id", employeeId.value()).query(Boolean.class).single());
    }

    @Override
    public Map<EmployeeId, EmployeeLabel> findLabels(Set<EmployeeId> employeeIds) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select id, display_name, employee_code from organization_workforce.employees where id = any(:ids)
                """)
                .param("ids", employeeIds.stream().map(EmployeeId::value).toArray(UUID[]::new))
                .query((rs, n) -> Map.entry(new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)),
                        new EmployeeLabel(rs.getString("display_name"), rs.getString("employee_code"))))
                .list().stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(Map.Entry::getKey, Map.Entry::getValue));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StaffEmployee> findStaffByWecomUser(WecomUserId wecomUserId) {
        return jdbc.sql(OrgSnapshotRows.SELECT_STAFF_ON_DAY + """
                 and s.wecom_user_id = :wecom order by (s.employment_status = 'EMPLOYED') desc, s.updated_at desc limit 1
                """)
                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("wecom", wecomUserId.value()).query((rs, n) -> OrgSnapshotRows.staff(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StaffEmployee> findStaffByLoginName(String loginName) {
        return jdbc.sql(OrgSnapshotRows.SELECT_STAFF_ON_DAY + """
                 and s.login_name = :login order by (s.employment_status = 'EMPLOYED') desc, s.updated_at desc limit 1
                """)
                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("login", loginName).query((rs, n) -> OrgSnapshotRows.staff(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public List<EmployeeId> findEmployeeIdsByLoginName(String loginName) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select id from organization_workforce.employees where login_name = :login order by updated_at desc, id
                """)
                .param("login", loginName).query(UUID.class).list().stream().map(EmployeeId::new).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<EmployeeId> findEmployeeIdsByWecomUser(WecomUserId wecomUserId) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select id from organization_workforce.employees where wecom_user_id = :wecom order by updated_at desc, id
                """)
                .param("wecom", wecomUserId.value()).query(UUID.class).list().stream().map(EmployeeId::new).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<OrgUnit> findOrgUnit(OrgUnitId orgUnitId) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select u.id, u.code, case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.name else u.name end as name,
                       u.parent_org_unit_id, u.sort_order, u.status,
                       case when exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.org_unit_versions x where x.org_unit_id = u.id) then v.head_employee_id
                            else u.head_employee_id end as head_employee_id,
                       u.regional_receivables_manager_id,
                       case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.region_kind else u.region_category end as region_category,
                       case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.is_delivery_unit else u.is_delivery_unit end as is_delivery_unit,
                       case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.short_name else u.short_name end as short_name
                  from organization_workforce.org_units u
                  left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions v on v.org_unit_id = u.id and v.superseded_at is null
                       and v.effective_from_date <= :day and (v.effective_to_date is null or :day < v.effective_to_date)
                 where u.id = :id
                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", orgUnitId.value()).query((rs, n) -> OrgSnapshotRows.orgUnit(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public List<OrgUnit> findAllOrgUnits() {
        return jdbc.sql(OrgSnapshotRows.SELECT_ORG_UNITS_ON_DAY).param("day", zone.today(clock)).query((rs, n) -> OrgSnapshotRows.orgUnit(rs)).list();
    }

    @Override
    public List<OrgUnitId> findAncestors(OrgUnitId orgUnitId) {
        List<UUID> chain = jdbc.sql("""
                with recursive chain(id, depth) as (
                    select parent_org_unit_id, 1 from organization_workforce.org_units
                     where id = :id and parent_org_unit_id is not null
                    union all
                    select u.parent_org_unit_id, c.depth + 1 from chain c
                      join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = c.id
                     where u.parent_org_unit_id is not null and c.depth < 64
                )
                select id from chain order by depth
                """).param("id", orgUnitId.value()).query(UUID.class).list();
        Set<UUID> unique = new LinkedHashSet<>(chain);
        unique.remove(orgUnitId.value());
        return unique.stream().map(OrgUnitId::new).toList();
    }
}

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;

@Repository
class JdbcLegalEntityQueries implements LegalEntityQueries {
    private static final String SELECT = """
            select id, code, name, status, version,
                   (select v.finance_head_employee_id from organization_workforce.legal_entity_versions v
                     where v.legal_entity_id = legal_entities.id and v.effective_to_date is null) as finance_head
              from organization_workforce.legal_entities
            """;

    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    JdbcLegalEntityQueries(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    @Override
    public List<LegalEntityRecord> findAll() {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + """
                 order by code
                """)
                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<LegalEntityRecord> find(LegalEntityId legalEntityId) {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + """
                 where id = :id
                """)
                .param("id", legalEntityId.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<LegalEntityRecord> findSharedLocked(LegalEntityId legalEntityId) {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + """
                 where id = :id
                   for share
                """)
                .param("id", legalEntityId.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
    }

    private static LegalEntityRecord row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        return new LegalEntityRecord(new LegalEntityId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), new LegalEntityCode(rs.getString("code")), rs.getString("name"),
                LegalEntityStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), rs.getLong("version"),
                Optional.ofNullable(rs.getObject("finance_head", UUID.class)).map(EmployeeId::new).orElse(null));
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeVersionQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Optional;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
class JdbcEmployeeVersionQueries implements EmployeeVersionQueries {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    JdbcEmployeeVersionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isOnLeave(EmployeeId employee, LocalDate day) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions
                 where employee_id = :employee and superseded_at is null and is_on_unpaid_leave
                   and effective_from_date <= :day and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
                """).param("employee", employee.value()).param("day", day).query(Integer.class).optional().isPresent();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasVersions(EmployeeId employee) {
        return jdbc.sql("select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions where employee_id = :employee limit 1")
                .param("employee", employee.value()).query(Integer.class).optional().isPresent();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<EmploymentStatus> employmentOn(EmployeeId employee, LocalDate day) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select employment_status from organization_workforce.employee_versions
                 where employee_id = :employee and superseded_at is null
                   and effective_from_date <= :day and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
                """).param("employee", employee.value()).param("day", day).query(String.class).optional().map(EmploymentStatus::valueOf);
    }
}
