/**
 * #730 S4, acceptance A1 to A4 of the fail-closed read paths (issue #730 comment 6597359528, security-invariants I-10): one generic harness over the
 * {@code SCOPED} lines of backend/architecture/data-scope-read-paths.txt. Every SCOPED line needs an adapter below (a line without one fails
 * {@link #everyScopedLineHasAnAdapter()}; so does an adapter whose line left the register). The harness seeds rows in unit A and unit B and
 * compares what a caller whose scope covers B only gets with what a caller of the whole organisation gets, so the expected rows come from the data
 * and not from the handler.
 *
 * <p>A1 lists give B's rows and none of A's through every filter and every cursor page; A2 a detail in A answers 404 with the body of an id that
 * does not exist; A3 an empty scope (a custom unit that names nothing, an own-unit scope with no assignment) gives zero rows and 404, never all
 * rows; A4 a scope that cannot be worked out gives 403 on lists and 404 on details, never a wider answer. Ruling: design comment 6597359528 (A4 as
 * worded there); the SCOPED lines are those of the register.
 */
class DataScopeReadBehaviourIT extends DataScopeSupport {

    static final TestDatabase postgres = TestPostgres.database(DataScopeReadBehaviourIT.class, TestPostgres.MIGRATED).withUrlParam("currentSchema", "platform_approval");

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void database(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        postgres.register(registry);
    }

    static final Path REGISTER = Path.of("architecture/data-scope-read-paths.txt");
    static final String PERSONNEL_COMMAND_CREATE = "organization.workforce:personnel-command:create";
    static final String REQUESTS = "/api/v1/organization/employee-profile-change-requests";
    static final String ORG_UNIT_ENTITIES = "/api/v1/organization/org-unit-legal-entities/{orgUnitId}";
    static final String EMPLOYEE_ENTITIES = "/api/v1/organization/employee-legal-entities/{employeeId}";
    static final String ORG_UNIT = "/api/v1/organization/org-units/{orgUnitId}";
    static final String ADJUSTMENTS = "/api/v1/platform/permission-adjustments";
    static final String ADJUSTMENT = ADJUSTMENTS + "/{permissionAdjustmentId}";

    /**
     * A4 does not apply to these lines: their scope is a relation (permission-adjustment:read holders, else recipient = caller), the list is always 200
     * and never 403, and the reader never calls OrgDirectory, so there is nothing that can fail to resolve (one-design, #730 comment 6602368110 point 3;
     * the harness cannot force it, the register line names its own proof). A1 to A3 still run on them.
     */
    static final Set<String> A4_EXEMPT = Set.of("GET " + ADJUSTMENTS, "GET " + ADJUSTMENT);

    /** The org unit of the row, or null for a row of no unit the world knows. */
    private record ListPath(String register, String path, List<String> queries, boolean paged, String idField, Function<Map<String, Object>, String> unitOfRow) {
    }

    private record DetailPath(String register, String inA, String inB) {
    }

    private final Map<String, String> unitIds = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, String> unitOfEmployee = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    /** Permission adjustments are scoped by relation, not by unit: "F1-B" stands for the items addressed to scopeB, "F1-A" for every other item. */
    private final Map<String, String> unitOfAdjustment = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    @MockitoSpyBean
    OrgDirectory organisation;

    /** Two scope reviews addressed to scopeB (the "B" rows) and, for the "A" rows, one addressed to another head and one sensitive review with no recipient. */
    private void seedAdjustments() throws Exception {
        OffsetDateTime at = OffsetDateTime.of(2026, 9, 1, 2, 0, 0, 0, ZoneOffset.UTC);
        UUID from = UUID.fromString(unitIds.get("F1-A"));
        UUID to = UUID.fromString(unitIds.get("F1-B"));
        int minute = 0;
        for (String[] item : new String[][] {{"a1", "a2", "F1-A"}, {"b1", "scopeB", "F1-B"}, {"b2", "scopeB", "F1-B"}, {"a2", null, "F1-A"}}) {
            UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
            OffsetDateTime created = at.plusMinutes(minute++);
            String kind = item[1] == null ? "SENSITIVE_ROLE_REVIEW" : "SCOPE_REVIEW";
            try (Connection identity = schemaConnection("platform_identity"); PreparedStatement statement = identity.prepareStatement("""
                    insert into permission_adjustments (permission_adjustment_id, kind, origin_kind, status, escalation_due_at, created_at, updated_at, user_id,
                        trigger_personnel_command_id, trigger_field_kind, before_org_unit_id, after_org_unit_id, recipient_employee_id)
                    values (?, ?, 'ASSIGNMENT_CHANGE', 'OPEN', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'ORG_UNIT', ?, ?, ?)""")) {
                statement.setObject(1, id);
                statement.setString(2, kind);
                statement.setObject(3, created.plusDays(7));
                statement.setObject(4, created);
                statement.setObject(5, created);
                statement.setObject(6, UUID.fromString(userId(item[0])));
                statement.setObject(7, UUID.randomUUID());
                statement.setObject(8, from);
                statement.setObject(9, to);
                statement.setObject(10, item[1] == null ? null : UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(item[1])));
                statement.executeUpdate();
            }
            unitOfAdjustment.put(id.toString(), item[2]);
        }
    }

    @AfterEach
    void organisationWorksAgain() {
        Mockito.reset(organisation);
    }

    /** A role that needs the second person when its permissions are sensitive: created at once (201) or confirmed by the second administrator (202 then 204). */
    private void roleWith(String code, String scope, List<CustomOrgUnit> units, String... permissions) throws Exception {
        MockHttpServletResponse created = createRole(administrator("admin1"), roleBody(code, scope, units, permissions));
        if (created.getStatus() == 202) {
            String request = JsonPath.read(created.getContentAsString(), "$.requestId");
            require(confirm(administrator("admin2"), request), 204, "confirming role " + code);
            eventually(() -> assertThat(roleIdOf(administrator("admin1"), code)).isPresent());
        } else {
            require(created, 201, "creating role " + code);
        }
    }

    // ---- the adapters: one per SCOPED line ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private List<ListPath> lists() {
        return List.of(
                new ListPath("GET /api/v1/organization/org-units", ORG_UNITS, List.of("", "?includeRevoked=true", "?includeRevoked=false"), false, "orgUnitId",
                        row -> unitCodeOf(String.valueOf(row.get("orgUnitId")))),
                new ListPath("GET " + REQUESTS, REQUESTS, List.of("?status=PENDING"), true, "requestId", row -> unitOfEmployee.get(String.valueOf(row.get("employeeId")))),
                new ListPath("GET " + EMPLOYEES, EMPLOYEES, List.of("", "?employmentStatus=ALL"), true, "employeeId", row -> unitOfEmployee.get(String.valueOf(row.get("employeeId")))),
                new ListPath("GET " + ADJUSTMENTS, ADJUSTMENTS, List.of(""), true, "permissionAdjustmentId",
                        row -> unitOfAdjustment.get(String.valueOf(row.get("permissionAdjustmentId")))));
    }

    private List<DetailPath> details() {
        return List.of(
                new DetailPath("GET " + ORG_UNIT, unitIds.get("F1-A"), unitIds.get("F1-B")),
                new DetailPath("GET " + ORG_UNIT_ENTITIES, unitIds.get("F1-A"), unitIds.get("F1-B")),
                new DetailPath("GET " + EMPLOYEE_ENTITIES, employeeIdOf("a1"), employeeIdOf("b1")),
                new DetailPath("GET " + EMPLOYEES + "/{employeeId}", employeeIdOf("a1"), employeeIdOf("b1")),
                new DetailPath("GET " + ADJUSTMENT, adjustmentIn("F1-A"), adjustmentIn("F1-B")));
    }

    private String adjustmentIn(String unit) {
        return unitOfAdjustment.entrySet().stream().filter(item -> item.getValue().equals(unit)).map(Map.Entry::getKey).findFirst().orElseThrow();
    }

    private String unitCodeOf(String unitId) {
        return unitIds.entrySet().stream().filter(unit -> unit.getValue().equals(unitId)).map(Map.Entry::getKey).findFirst().orElse(null);
    }

    private static String urlOf(String registerLine, String id) {
        return registerLine.substring("GET ".length()).replaceAll("\\{[A-Za-z]+}", id);
    }

    // ---- the register -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static Set<String> scopedLines() throws IOException {
        Set<String> lines = new TreeSet<>();
        for (String line : Files.readAllLines(REGISTER)) {
            if (line.startsWith("GET ")) {
                String[] parts = line.split("\\|");
                if (parts[1].trim().equals("SCOPED")) {
                    lines.add(parts[0].trim());
                }
            }
        }
        return lines;
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A1-A4: every SCOPED line of the register has an adapter here, and every adapter is a SCOPED line")
    void everyScopedLineHasAnAdapter() throws Exception {
        Set<String> adapted = new TreeSet<>();
        lists().forEach(list -> adapted.add(list.register()));
        details().forEach(detail -> adapted.add(detail.register()));
        assertThat(adapted).containsExactlyInAnyOrderElementsOf(scopedLines());
        assertThat(scopedLines()).as("an A4 exemption names a SCOPED line of the register").containsAll(A4_EXEMPT);
    }

    // ---- calls --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private MockHttpServletResponse read(String key, String url) throws Exception {
        return mvc.perform(get(url).with(as(session(key)))).andReturn().getResponse();
    }

    /** Every row of the list as the caller gets it: all cursor pages of a paged list, taken one row per page so that a filter applied after the page shows. */
    private List<Map<String, Object>> rowsOf(String key, ListPath list, String query) throws Exception {
        List<Map<String, Object>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        String cursor = null;
        for (int page = 0; page < 20; page++) {
            String url = list.path() + query;
            if (list.paged()) {
                url += (url.contains("?") ? "&" : "?") + "limit=1" + (cursor == null ? "" : "&cursor=" + cursor);
            }
            MockHttpServletResponse answer = read(key, url);
            require(answer, 200, key + " reads " + url);
            rows.addAll(items(answer));
            cursor = list.paged() ? JsonPath.read(answer.getContentAsString(), "$.nextCursor") : null;
            if (cursor == null) {
                return rows;
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError(key + " reads " + list.path() + query + ": more than 20 pages of one row");
    }

    private static Set<String> idsOf(List<Map<String, Object>> rows, ListPath list) {
        Set<String> ids = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        rows.forEach(row -> ids.add(String.valueOf(row.get(list.idField()))));
        return ids;
    }

    /** The body of a 404 without the id the request named, so that a row that exists elsewhere cannot be told from one that does not exist. */
    private static String refusalBody(MockHttpServletResponse answer, String id) throws Exception {
        Map<String, Object> body = JsonPath.parse(answer.getContentAsString()).read("$");
        body = new LinkedHashMap<>(body);
        body.keySet().removeAll(Set.of("correlationId"));
        return body.toString().replace(id, "<id>");
    }

    private void assertAbsentAnswer(String key, DetailPath detail, String id, String what) throws Exception {
        String absent = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        MockHttpServletResponse nothing = read(key, urlOf(detail.register(), absent));
        MockHttpServletResponse refused = read(key, urlOf(detail.register(), id));
        assertThat(nothing.getStatus()).as(key + " reads " + detail.register() + " for an id that does not exist").isEqualTo(404);
        assertThat(refused.getStatus()).as(what + ": " + refused.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(404);
        assertThat(refusalBody(refused, id)).as(what + " answers like an id that does not exist").isEqualTo(refusalBody(nothing, absent));
    }

    // ---- A1 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A1: a caller whose scope covers unit B only gets B's rows and none of A's from every SCOPED list, through every filter and cursor page")
    void listsGiveTheRowsOfUnitBOnly() throws Exception {
        for (ListPath list : lists()) {
            for (String query : list.queries()) {
                List<Map<String, Object>> everything = rowsOf("everything", list, query);
                Set<String> inB = new LinkedHashSet<>();
                Set<String> inA = new LinkedHashSet<>();
                for (Map<String, Object> row : everything) {
                    String unit = list.unitOfRow().apply(row);
                    (("F1-B".equals(unit)) ? inB : "F1-A".equals(unit) ? inA : new LinkedHashSet<String>()).add(String.valueOf(row.get(list.idField())));
                }
                assertThat(inA).as("the seed: " + list.register() + query + " has rows in A").isNotEmpty();
                assertThat(inB).as("the seed: " + list.register() + query + " has rows in B").isNotEmpty();
                assertThat(idsOf(rowsOf("scopeB", list, query), list)).as(list.register() + query + " for a caller of unit B alone").containsExactlyInAnyOrderElementsOf(inB);
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- A2 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A2: a SCOPED detail of unit A is 404 for a caller of unit B alone, with the body of an id that does not exist; the detail of B is readable")
    void detailsOutOfScopeAreNotFound() throws Exception {
        for (DetailPath detail : details()) {
            assertThat(read("everything", urlOf(detail.register(), detail.inA())).getStatus()).as("the seed: " + detail.register() + " reads A for the whole organisation").isEqualTo(200);
            assertAbsentAnswer("scopeB", detail, detail.inA(), detail.register() + " for A");
            assertThat(read("scopeB", urlOf(detail.register(), detail.inB())).getStatus()).as(detail.register() + " for B").isEqualTo(200);
        }
    }

    // ---- A3 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A3: a caller whose scope is empty (a custom unit that names nothing; an own-unit scope with no assignment) gets zero rows and 404, never all rows")
    void anEmptyScopeSeesNothing() throws Exception {
        require(read("nohome", ORG_UNITS), 200, "priming the session of the caller: a person without an assignment cannot sign in, so the assignment goes after the sign-in");
        removeAssignmentOf("nohome");
        for (String key : List.of("dangling", "nohome")) {
            for (ListPath list : lists()) {
                for (String query : list.queries()) {
                    assertThat(rowsOf(key, list, query)).as(key + " reads " + list.register() + query).isEmpty();
                }
            }
            for (DetailPath detail : details()) {
                assertAbsentAnswer(key, detail, detail.inA(), key + " reads " + detail.register() + " for A");
                assertAbsentAnswer(key, detail, detail.inB(), key + " reads " + detail.register() + " for B");
            }
        }
    }

    private void removeAssignmentOf(String key) throws Exception {
        try (Connection workforce = schemaConnection("organization_workforce");
                PreparedStatement statement = workforce.prepareStatement("delete from assignments where employee_id = ?")) {
            statement.setObject(1, UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(key)));
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    // ---- A4 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A4: a caller whose scope cannot be worked out (the organisation cannot answer who the person is) gets 403 on lists and 404 on details, never more")
    void anUnresolvableScopeIsDenied() throws Exception {
        String employee = employeeIdOf("broken");
        require(read("broken", ORG_UNITS), 200, "priming the principal of the caller before the organisation fails (the principal is cached for a minute)");
        doThrow(new IllegalStateException("organisation unavailable")).when(organisation).findEmployee(argThat(id -> id != null && id.value().toString().equals(employee)));
        for (ListPath list : lists()) {
            if (A4_EXEMPT.contains(list.register())) {
                continue;
            }
            for (String query : list.queries()) {
                String url = list.path() + query;
                MockHttpServletResponse refused = read("broken", url);
                assertThat(refused.getStatus()).as(url + ": " + refused.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(403);
            }
        }
        for (DetailPath detail : details()) {
            if (A4_EXEMPT.contains(detail.register())) {
                continue;
            }
            for (String id : List.of(detail.inA(), detail.inB())) {
                MockHttpServletResponse refused = read("broken", urlOf(detail.register(), id));
                assertThat(refused.getStatus()).as(detail.register() + ": " + refused.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(404);
            }
        }
    }
}

    @Test

        List<Map<String, Object>> assignments = JsonPath.parse(multi).read("$.assignments");

        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("assignmentKind")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("PRIMARY", "CONCURRENT");
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("orgUnitId")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(unitIds.get("RS-C"), unitIds.get("RS-B"));
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("orgUnitName")).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(unitName("RS-C"), unitName("RS-B"));
        assertThat(assignments).extracting(item -> item.get("positionName")).containsOnly(positionName("RS-P2"));
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("cReader", ""))).containsExactly("c", "multi");
        assertThat(worldKeys(all("subtreeReader", ""))).doesNotContain("multi");
    }

        assertThat(all("subtreeReader", "orgUnitId=" + unitIds.get("RS-C"))).as("the filter cannot widen the data scope").isEmpty();
    }

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.web;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ExpectedVersion;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.LegalEntityId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.AttributionKind;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.AttributeLegalEntity;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.CorrectLegalEntityAttribution;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.EndLegalEntityAttribution;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.GetLegalEntityAttribution;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.LegalEntityAttributionView;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import java.util.UUID;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization")
class LegalEntityAttributionController {
    private final GetLegalEntityAttribution get;
    private final AttributeLegalEntity attribute;
    private final EndLegalEntityAttribution end;
    private final CorrectLegalEntityAttribution correct;

    LegalEntityAttributionController(GetLegalEntityAttribution get, AttributeLegalEntity attribute, EndLegalEntityAttribution end,
            CorrectLegalEntityAttribution correct) {
        this.correct = correct;
        this.get = get;
        this.attribute = attribute;
        this.end = end;
    }

    @GetMapping("/org-unit-legal-entities/{orgUnitId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_UNIT_READ + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> ofOrgUnit(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId) {
        return answer(get.execute(AttributionKind.ORG_UNIT, orgUnitId));
    }

    @PutMapping("/org-unit-legal-entities/{orgUnitId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_UPDATE + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> attributeOrgUnit(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId, @RequestBody AttributeLegalEntityRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(attribute.execute(AttributionKind.ORG_UNIT, orgUnitId, request.legalEntityId() == null ? null : new LegalEntityId(request.legalEntityId()), request.effectiveFrom(),
                ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    @PostMapping("/org-unit-legal-entities/{orgUnitId}:end")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_UPDATE + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> endOrgUnit(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId, @RequestBody EndLegalEntityAttributionRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(end.execute(AttributionKind.ORG_UNIT, orgUnitId, request.effectiveTo(), ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    @GetMapping("/employee-legal-entities/{employeeId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_UNIT_READ + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> ofEmployee(@PathVariable UUID employeeId) {
        return answer(get.execute(AttributionKind.EMPLOYEE, employeeId));
    }

    @PutMapping("/employee-legal-entities/{employeeId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_UPDATE + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> attributeEmployee(@PathVariable UUID employeeId, @RequestBody AttributeLegalEntityRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(attribute.execute(AttributionKind.EMPLOYEE, employeeId, request.legalEntityId() == null ? null : new LegalEntityId(request.legalEntityId()), request.effectiveFrom(),
                ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    @PostMapping("/employee-legal-entities/{employeeId}:end")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_UPDATE + "')")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> endEmployee(@PathVariable UUID employeeId, @RequestBody EndLegalEntityAttributionRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(end.execute(AttributionKind.EMPLOYEE, employeeId, request.effectiveTo(), ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    @PostMapping("/org-unit-legal-entities/{orgUnitId}:correct")
    @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> correctOrgUnit(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId, @RequestBody CorrectLegalEntityAttributionRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(correct.execute(AttributionKind.ORG_UNIT, orgUnitId, request.periodFrom(), request.effectiveTo(), request.reason(),
                ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    @PostMapping("/employee-legal-entities/{employeeId}:correct")
    @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
    ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> correctEmployee(@PathVariable UUID employeeId, @RequestBody CorrectLegalEntityAttributionRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(value = "If-Match", required = false) String ifMatch) {
        return answer(correct.execute(AttributionKind.EMPLOYEE, employeeId, request.periodFrom(), request.effectiveTo(), request.reason(),
                ExpectedVersion.fromHeader(ifMatch)));
    }

    private static ResponseEntity<LegalEntityAttributionResponse> answer(LegalEntityAttributionView view) {
        ResponseEntity.BodyBuilder ok = ResponseEntity.ok();
        ok.eTag(ExpectedVersion.etag(view.version() == null ? 0 : view.version()));
        return ok.body(LegalEntityAttributionResponse.of(view));
    }
}

/** The decider's reads of a legal-entity countersign and the merge rehearsal (#910 amendment B; paths ruled by one-design on #910, comment 6588705997; the basis path is flat by lead ruling, comment 6611676984, because G41 allows no sub-resource). */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization")
class CountersignReadController {
    private final AttributionCountersigns countersigns;
    private final GetAttributionCountersign get;
    private final ListAttributionCountersignBases listBases;
    private final PreviewMergeAttribution previewMerge;

    CountersignReadController(AttributionCountersigns countersigns, GetAttributionCountersign get, ListAttributionCountersignBases listBases, PreviewMergeAttribution previewMerge) {
        this.countersigns = countersigns;
        this.get = get;
        this.listBases = listBases;
        this.previewMerge = previewMerge;
    }

    /** The requests the caller may decide; a status other than the four is a 400 by the enum binding, and none given means OPEN. */
    @GetMapping("/attribution-countersigns")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_ATTRIBUTION_APPROVE + "')")
    CursorPage<SummaryResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) AttributionCountersignStatus status, @RequestParam(required = false) Integer limit,
                                     @RequestParam(required = false) String cursor) {
        CursorPage<AttributionCountersignSummary> page = countersigns.list(status, PageRequest.of(limit, cursor, "attribution-countersigns"));
        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(SummaryResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
    }

    @GetMapping("/attribution-countersigns/{attributionCountersignId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_ATTRIBUTION_APPROVE + "')")
    RowResponse row(@PathVariable UUID attributionCountersignId) {
        return RowResponse.of(get.execute(new AttributionCountersignId(attributionCountersignId)));
    }

    @GetMapping("/attribution-countersign-bases/{orgStructureCommandId}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.LEGAL_ENTITY_ATTRIBUTION_APPROVE + "', '" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_STRUCTURE_COMMAND_CREATE + "')")
    CursorPage<BasisLineResponse> bases(@PathVariable UUID orgStructureCommandId, @RequestParam(required = false) Integer limit, @RequestParam(required = false) String cursor) {
        CursorPage<BasisLine> page = listBases.execute(new OrgStructureCommandId(orgStructureCommandId), limit, cursor);
        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(BasisLineResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
    }

    @GetMapping("/org-units/{orgUnitId}:preview-merge")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.ORG_STRUCTURE_COMMAND_CREATE + "')")
    MergePreviewResponse previewMerge(@PathVariable UUID orgUnitId, @RequestParam UUID mergeIntoOrgUnitId, @RequestParam LocalDate effectiveDate) {
        return new MergePreviewResponse(previewMerge.execute(new OrgUnitId(orgUnitId), new OrgUnitId(mergeIntoOrgUnitId), effectiveDate).stream().map(MergeLineResponse::of).toList());
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.LegalEntityId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.AttributionSubject;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.AttributionPeriod;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.LegalEntityAttributionQueries;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
class JdbcLegalEntityAttributionQueries implements LegalEntityAttributionQueries {
    private final JdbcClient jdbc;

    JdbcLegalEntityAttributionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
    }

    @Override
    public java.util.Optional<Long> versionOf(AttributionSubject subject) {
        return jdbc.sql("""
                select version from organization_workforce.legal_entity_attributions where
                """ + EffectiveAttributionPeriods.subjectColumn(subject.kind()) + """
                 = :id
                """)
                .param("id", subject.subjectId()).query(Long.class).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public List<AttributionPeriod> periodsOf(AttributionSubject subject) {
        return jdbc.sql(EffectiveAttributionPeriods.select(subject.kind()) + """
                 order by p.effective_from_date
                """)
                .param("id", subject.subjectId())
                .query((rs, n) -> new AttributionPeriod(new LegalEntityId(rs.getObject("legal_entity_id", UUID.class)),
                        rs.getObject("effective_from_date", LocalDate.class),
                        rs.getObject("effective_to_date", LocalDate.class))).list();
    }
}

/**
 * #1171, the employee roster: {@code GET /api/v1/organization/employees?orgUnitId=&employmentStatus=&limit=&cursor=} and {@code .../employees/{employeeId}}
 * for the holders of {@code organization.workforce:employee:read}. The code is held by the system role personnel_specialist only, not by super_admin,
 * org_data_admin or any other (user 2026-11-03, intent 214823); the list is filtered by the caller's data scope, fail-closed; default lists employed
 * people only, {@code employmentStatus=ALL} or {@code SEPARATED} include the separated (same intent); a person with several assignments is in scope when
 * any assignment's department is in scope and the row lists every assignment; the detail lists all assignments with the reports-to person (user
 * 2026-11-04, intent 055504); an out-of-scope id answers 404 and writes the audit entry organization.workforce.employee.denied; no ID-card number,
 * ethnicity or household registration anywhere (ADR-0079:124, ADR-0080 U-14).
 *
 * <p>Contract fixed here, because the card names no field. A row: {@code employeeId, employeeCode, displayName, employmentStatus, hireDate,
 * assignments[]}; an assignment: {@code assignmentKind (PRIMARY|CONCURRENT), orgUnitId, orgUnitName, positionId, positionName}; the detail adds
 * {@code reportsToEmployeeId, reportsToDisplayName} to each assignment (null on a concurrent one). A page is {@code items} and {@code nextCursor}. A scope
 * that cannot be worked out answers 403 on the list and 404 on the detail (one-design, #730 comment 6597359528, as DataScopeReadBehaviourIT). The
 * roster is read from the versions (employee versions for status and hire date, assignment versions for unit, position and reporting line; the
 * concurrent assignment exists only there). The snapshot rows are written too, only because sign-in and scope resolution need them; both agree on unit and position.
 */
class EmployeeRosterHttpIT extends DataScopeSupport {

    static final TestDatabase postgres = TestPostgres.database(EmployeeRosterHttpIT.class, TestPostgres.MIGRATED).withUrlParam("currentSchema", "platform_approval");

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void database(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        postgres.register(registry);
    }

    static final String DENIED = "organization.workforce.employee.denied";
    static final LocalDate STARTED = LocalDate.of(2025, 3, 1);
    static final LocalDate STARTED_SUB = LocalDate.of(2024, 7, 15);
    static final LocalDate SINCE = LocalDate.of(2026, 1, 1);
    static final List<String> WORLD = List.of("a", "sub", "a1", "b", "c", "multi", "gone");
    static final Set<String> ROW_KEYS = Set.of("employeeId", "employeeCode", "displayName", "employmentStatus", "hireDate", "assignments");
    static final Set<String> ASSIGNMENT_KEYS = Set.of("assignmentKind", "orgUnitId", "orgUnitName", "positionId", "positionName", "reportsToEmployeeId", "reportsToDisplayName");
    static final Pattern FORBIDDEN_KEY = Pattern.compile("(?i)id.?card|idnumber|identity.?(card|number)|ethnic|household|national.?id");

    private final Map<String, String> unitIds = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    @MockitoSpyBean
    OrgDirectory organisation;

    @BeforeAll
    void theWorld() throws Exception {
        unit("RS-A", null);
        unit("RS-A1", "RS-A");
        unit("RS-B", null);
        unit("RS-C", null);
        unit("RS-X", null);
        position("RS-P1");
        position("RS-P2");
        for (String key : List.of("a", "sub", "gone", "ownReader")) {
            person(key, "RS-A", "RS-P1");
        }
        person("a1", "RS-A1", "RS-P1");
        person("b", "RS-B", "RS-P1");
        person("c", "RS-C", "RS-P2");
        person("multi", "RS-C", "RS-P2");
        for (String key : List.of("admin1", "admin2", "hr", "oda", "allReader", "subtreeReader", "bReader", "emptyReader", "brokenReader", "orgOnly", "cReader")) {
            person(key, "RS-X", "RS-P1");
        }
        seedOrganisation();
        administrator("admin1");
        administrator("admin2");
        grantByOperations("oda", ORG_DATA_ADMIN);
        grantByOperations("hr", "personnel_specialist");
        for (String code : List.of("RS-A", "RS-A1", "RS-B", "RS-C", "RS-X")) {
            unitIds.put(code, orgUnitId(session("oda"), code));
        }
        String[] perms = {EMPLOYEE_READ};
        roleWith("rs-all", "ALL", null, perms);
        roleWith("rs-subtree-a", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(withDescendants(unitIds.get("RS-A"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-only-b", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(unitIds.get("RS-B"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-only-c", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(unitIds.get("RS-C"))), perms);
        roleWith("rs-nothing", "CUSTOM_ORG_UNITS", List.of(onlyUnit(UUID.randomUUID().toString())), perms);
    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A3: a caller whose scope is empty (a custom unit that names nothing; an own-unit scope with no assignment) gets zero rows and 404, never all rows")
    void anEmptyScopeSeesNothing() throws Exception {
        require(read("nohome", ORG_UNITS), 200, "priming the session of the caller: a person without an assignment cannot sign in, so the assignment goes after the sign-in");
        removeAssignmentOf("nohome");
        for (String key : List.of("dangling", "nohome")) {
            for (ListPath list : lists()) {
                for (String query : list.queries()) {
                    assertThat(rowsOf(key, list, query)).as(key + " reads " + list.register() + query).isEmpty();
                }
            }
            for (DetailPath detail : details()) {
                assertAbsentAnswer(key, detail, detail.inA(), key + " reads " + detail.register() + " for A");
                assertAbsentAnswer(key, detail, detail.inB(), key + " reads " + detail.register() + " for B");
            }
        }
    }

    private void removeAssignmentOf(String key) throws Exception {
        try (Connection workforce = schemaConnection("organization_workforce");
                PreparedStatement statement = workforce.prepareStatement("delete from assignments where employee_id = ?")) {
            statement.setObject(1, UUID.fromString(employeeIdOf(key)));
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    // ---- A4 ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("#730 A4: a caller whose scope cannot be worked out (the organisation cannot answer who the person is) gets 403 on lists and 404 on details, never more")
    void anUnresolvableScopeIsDenied() throws Exception {
        String employee = employeeIdOf("broken");
        require(read("broken", ORG_UNITS), 200, "priming the principal of the caller before the organisation fails (the principal is cached for a minute)");
        doThrow(new IllegalStateException("organisation unavailable")).when(organisation).findEmployee(argThat(id -> id != null && id.value().toString().equals(employee)));
        for (ListPath list : lists()) {
            if (A4_EXEMPT.contains(list.register())) {
                continue;
            }
            for (String query : list.queries()) {
                String url = list.path() + query;
                MockHttpServletResponse refused = read("broken", url);
                assertThat(refused.getStatus()).as(url + ": " + refused.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(403);
            }
        }
        for (DetailPath detail : details()) {
            if (A4_EXEMPT.contains(detail.register())) {
                continue;
            }
            for (String id : List.of(detail.inA(), detail.inB())) {
                MockHttpServletResponse refused = read("broken", urlOf(detail.register(), id));
                assertThat(refused.getStatus()).as(detail.register() + ": " + refused.getContentAsString()).isEqualTo(404);
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.WecomUserId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.Assignment;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.Employee;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.Gender;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnit;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgUnitStatus;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;

/** The SELECTs and row mappers of the org snapshot: the repository loads the snapshot rows for the reconciliation, the queries browse as of a business day. */
final class OrgSnapshotRows {
    private OrgSnapshotRows() {
    }

    static final String SELECT_ORG_UNITS = """
            select id, code, name, parent_org_unit_id, sort_order, status, head_employee_id,
                   regional_receivables_manager_id, region_category, is_delivery_unit, short_name
              from organization_workforce.org_units
            """;

    static final String SELECT_ORG_UNITS_ON_DAY = """
            select u.id, u.code, case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.name else u.name end as name,
                   u.parent_org_unit_id, u.sort_order, u.status, u.head_employee_id, u.regional_receivables_manager_id,
                   case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.region_kind else u.region_category end as region_category,
                   case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.is_delivery_unit else u.is_delivery_unit end as is_delivery_unit,
                   case when v.org_unit_version_id is not null then v.short_name else u.short_name end as short_name
              from organization_workforce.org_units u
              left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions v on v.org_unit_id = u.id and v.superseded_at is null
                   and v.effective_from_date <= :day and (v.effective_to_date is null or :day < v.effective_to_date)
            """;

    /** The snapshot rows as stored; for the reconciliation's own comparison only, readers use SELECT_STAFF_ON_DAY. */
    static final String SELECT_STAFF = """
            select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.login_name, e.email, e.gender, e.wecom_user_id,
                   e.employment_status, e.is_fee_earner,
                   a.org_unit_id, a.position_id, a.reports_to_employee_id
              from organization_workforce.employees e
              join organization_workforce.assignments a on a.employee_id = e.id
            """;

    /**
     * The staff row as of {@code :day}. A person with any employee version is read from the versions in force that day (status and fee-earner flag from the
     * employee version, org unit, position and reporting line from the assignment version) and is absent when either is missing; everyone else from the
     * snapshot. Name, gender, email, WeCom account and code stay on the root row.
     */
    static final String SELECT_STAFF_ON_DAY = """
            select s.* from (
              select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.login_name, e.email, e.gender, e.wecom_user_id, e.updated_at,
                     case when v.versioned then ev.employment_status else e.employment_status end as employment_status,
                     case when v.versioned then ev.is_fee_earner else e.is_fee_earner end as is_fee_earner,
                     case when v.versioned then av.org_unit_id else a.org_unit_id end as org_unit_id,
                     case when v.versioned then av.position_id else a.position_id end as position_id,
                     case when v.versioned then av.reports_to_employee_id else a.reports_to_employee_id end as reports_to_employee_id
                from organization_workforce.employees e
                cross join lateral (select exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = e.id) as versioned) v
                left join organization_workforce.employee_versions ev on v.versioned and ev.employee_id = e.id and ev.superseded_at is null
                     and ev.effective_from_date <= :day and (ev.effective_to_date is null or :day < ev.effective_to_date)
                left join organization_workforce.assignment_versions av on v.versioned and av.employee_id = e.id and av.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' and av.superseded_at is null
                     and av.effective_from_date <= :day and (av.effective_to_date is null or :day < av.effective_to_date)
                left join organization_workforce.assignments a on not v.versioned and a.employee_id = e.id
            ) s
            where s.org_unit_id is not null and s.employment_status is not null
            """;

    static OrgUnit orgUnit(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        return new OrgUnit(new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("code"), rs.getString("name"),
                orgUnitId(rs, "parent_org_unit_id"), integer(rs, "sort_order"),
                OrgUnitStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), employeeId(rs, "head_employee_id"),
                employeeId(rs, "regional_receivables_manager_id"),
                rs.getString("region_category") == null ? null : RegionCategory.valueOf(rs.getString("region_category")),
                rs.getBoolean("is_delivery_unit"), rs.getString("short_name"));
    }

    static StaffEmployee staff(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        EmployeeId id = new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class));
        String wecom = rs.getString("wecom_user_id");
        String gender = rs.getString("gender");
        Employee employee = new Employee(id, rs.getString("employee_code"), rs.getString("display_name"),
                rs.getString("login_name"), rs.getString("email"), gender == null ? null : Gender.valueOf(gender),
                wecom == null ? null : new WecomUserId(wecom), EmploymentStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("employment_status")),
                rs.getBoolean("is_fee_earner"));
        OrgUnitId orgUnit = new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("org_unit_id", UUID.class));
        UUID position = rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class);
        PositionId positionId = position == null ? null : new PositionId(position);
        Assignment assignment = new Assignment(id, orgUnit, positionId, employeeId(rs, "reports_to_employee_id"));
        return new StaffEmployee(employee, assignment);
    }

    static Integer integer(ResultSet rs, String column) throws SQLException {
        int value = rs.getInt(column);
        return rs.wasNull() ? null : value;
    }

    static EmployeeId employeeId(ResultSet rs, String column) throws SQLException {
        UUID value = rs.getObject(column, UUID.class);
        return value == null ? null : new EmployeeId(value);
    }

    static OrgUnitId orgUnitId(ResultSet rs, String column) throws SQLException {
        UUID value = rs.getObject(column, UUID.class);
        return value == null ? null : new OrgUnitId(value);
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.LegalEntityId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.AttributionCountersignId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PersonnelCommandId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.AttributionCountersignQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.CountersignStatus;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.OffsetDateTime;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Function;
import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

@Repository
class JdbcAttributionCountersignQueries implements AttributionCountersignQueries {
    /**
     * A personnel command carries the employee and the legal entities the countersign is about; an org-structure command carries neither (they differ per employee), so
     * those columns stay empty. The previous org unit is the one the assignment holds the day before the command takes effect.
     */
    private static final String SELECT = """
            select c.attribution_countersign_id as id, c.personnel_command_id,
                   coalesce(pc.command_kind, oc.command_kind) as command_kind, coalesce(pc.status, oc.status) as command_status,
                   coalesce(pc.effective_date, oc.effective_date) as effective_date,
                   c.employee_id, emp.display_name as employee_display_name, emp.employee_code as employee_number,
                   c.request_kind, c.request_sequence_number, c.decision_kind,
                   c.legal_entity_id, le.name as legal_entity_name,
                   pc.countersign_previous_legal_entity_id as previous_legal_entity_id, ple.name as previous_legal_entity_name,
                   pc.countersign_new_legal_entity_id as new_legal_entity_id, nle.name as new_legal_entity_name,
                   c.requested_at, c.requested_by, c.due_on_date, c.escalation_level_number,
                   previous_unit.id as previous_org_unit_id,
                   coalesce(previous_version.name, previous_unit.name) as previous_org_unit_name,
                   new_unit.id as new_org_unit_id,
                   coalesce(new_version.name, new_unit.name) as new_org_unit_name,
                   c.request_reason, c.decided_at, c.decided_by, c.decision_reason
              from organization_workforce.attribution_countersigns c
              join organization_workforce.legal_entities le on le.id = c.legal_entity_id
              left join organization_workforce.personnel_commands pc on pc.personnel_command_id = c.personnel_command_id
              left join organization_workforce.org_structure_commands oc on oc.org_structure_command_id = c.org_structure_command_id
              left join organization_workforce.employees emp on emp.id = c.employee_id
              left join organization_workforce.legal_entities ple on ple.id = pc.countersign_previous_legal_entity_id
              left join organization_workforce.legal_entities nle on nle.id = pc.countersign_new_legal_entity_id
              left join organization_workforce.org_units new_unit on new_unit.id = pc.target_org_unit_id
              left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions new_version on new_version.org_unit_id = new_unit.id and new_version.superseded_at is null
                   and new_version.effective_from_date <= :today and (new_version.effective_to_date is null or :today < new_version.effective_to_date)
              left join lateral (select u.id, u.name
                                   from organization_workforce.assignment_versions av join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = av.org_unit_id
                                  where pc.target_org_unit_id is not null and av.assignment_id = pc.target_assignment_id
                                    and av.effective_from_date < pc.effective_date and (av.effective_to_date is null or pc.effective_date - 1 < av.effective_to_date)
                                  order by av.effective_from_date desc, av.revision_number desc limit 1) previous_unit on true
              left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions previous_version on previous_version.org_unit_id = previous_unit.id and previous_version.superseded_at is null
                   and previous_version.effective_from_date <= :today and (previous_version.effective_to_date is null or :today < previous_version.effective_to_date)
            """;

    /** The alias is one of ours, never input; the same filter guards the row a cursor points at, so a cursor cannot probe a row the caller may not see. */
    private static final String VISIBLE = """
            (%1$s.legal_entity_id in (select v.legal_entity_id from organization_workforce.legal_entity_versions v
                                       where v.finance_head_employee_id = :head and v.effective_from_date <= :today and (v.effective_to_date is null or :today < v.effective_to_date))
             or (:escalated and %1$s.escalation_level_number >= 2))
            """;

    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final BusinessZone zone;

    JdbcAttributionCountersignQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
        this.jdbc = jdbc;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.zone = zone;
    }

    @Override
    public List<Row> list(Visible visible, CountersignStatus status, @Nullable UUID after, int limit) {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + " where " + VISIBLE.formatted("c") + """
                   and ((cast(:decision as text) is null and c.decision_kind is null) or c.decision_kind = cast(:decision as text))
                   and (cast(:after as uuid) is null or (c.due_on_date, c.attribution_countersign_id) > (select k.due_on_date, k.attribution_countersign_id
                                                                                              from organization_workforce.attribution_countersigns k
                                                                                             where k.attribution_countersign_id = cast(:after as uuid) and
                """ + VISIBLE.formatted("k") + """
                   ))
                 order by c.due_on_date, c.attribution_countersign_id
                 limit :limit
                """)
                .param("head", visible.head().value()).param("today", visible.today()).param("escalated", visible.escalated()).param("decision", status == CountersignStatus.OPEN ? null : status.name(), java.sql.Types.VARCHAR)
                .param("after", after, java.sql.Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit)
                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, AttributionCountersignId id) {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + " where c.attribution_countersign_id = :id and " + VISIBLE.formatted("c"))
                .param("id", id.value()).param("head", visible.head().value()).param("today", visible.today()).param("escalated", visible.escalated())
                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
    }

    @Override
    public List<Row> sameCommand(AttributionCountersignId id) {
        return jdbc.sql(SELECT + """
                  join organization_workforce.attribution_countersigns s on s.attribution_countersign_id = :id
                   and c.personnel_command_id is not distinct from s.personnel_command_id and c.org_structure_command_id is not distinct from s.org_structure_command_id
                 order by c.requested_at, c.request_kind, c.request_sequence_number, c.attribution_countersign_id
                """)
                .param("id", id.value()).param("today", zone.today(clock)).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
    }

    @Override
    public Map<UUID, String> employeeNames(Collection<UUID> employeeIds) {
        if (employeeIds.isEmpty()) {
            return Map.of();
        }
        Map<UUID, String> names = new HashMap<>();
        jdbc.sql("select id, display_name from organization_workforce.employees where id in (:ids)").param("ids", employeeIds)
                .query((rs, n) -> names.put(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class), rs.getString("display_name"))).list();
        return names;
    }

    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
        return new Row(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class), typed(rs, "personnel_command_id", PersonnelCommandId::new), rs.getString("command_kind"), rs.getString("command_status"),
                rs.getObject("effective_date", LocalDate.class), typed(rs, "employee_id", EmployeeId::new), rs.getString("employee_display_name"),
                rs.getString("employee_number"), rs.getString("request_kind"), rs.getInt("request_sequence_number"),
                CountersignStatus.ofDecision(rs.getString("decision_kind")), new LegalEntityId(rs.getObject("legal_entity_id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("legal_entity_name"),
                typed(rs, "previous_legal_entity_id", LegalEntityId::new), rs.getString("previous_legal_entity_name"), typed(rs, "new_legal_entity_id", LegalEntityId::new),
                rs.getString("new_legal_entity_name"), instant(rs, "requested_at"), rs.getObject("requested_by", UUID.class), rs.getObject("due_on_date", LocalDate.class),
                rs.getInt("escalation_level_number"), typed(rs, "previous_org_unit_id", OrgUnitId::new), rs.getString("previous_org_unit_name"),
                typed(rs, "new_org_unit_id", OrgUnitId::new), rs.getString("new_org_unit_name"), rs.getString("request_reason"), nullableInstant(rs, "decided_at"),
                rs.getObject("decided_by", UUID.class), rs.getString("decision_reason"));
    }

    private static <T> @Nullable T typed(ResultSet rs, String column, Function<UUID, T> wrap) throws SQLException {
        UUID value = rs.getObject(column, UUID.class);
        return value == null ? null : wrap.apply(value);
    }

    private static Instant instant(ResultSet rs, String column) throws SQLException {
        return rs.getObject(column, OffsetDateTime.class).toInstant();
    }

    private static @Nullable Instant nullableInstant(ResultSet rs, String column) throws SQLException {
        OffsetDateTime at = rs.getObject(column, OffsetDateTime.class);
        return at == null ? null : at.toInstant();
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api;

import java.util.Locale;

public enum AuditSubjectKind {
    USER_ACCOUNT(true),
    EMPLOYEE(true),
    ORG_UNIT(true),
    EMERGENCY_ADMIN(true),
    ROLE(true),
    APPROVAL_TASK(true),
    APPROVAL_REQUEST(true),
    SYNC_RUN(true),
    LEGAL_ENTITY(true),
    LEGAL_ENTITY_ATTRIBUTION(true),
    ORG_STRUCTURE_COMMAND(true),
    TIMESHEET(true),
    WORK_CALENDAR_YEAR(true),
    NUMBERING_RULE(true),
    SOURCE_REFERENCE(true),
    SOURCE_REFERENCE_REFUSAL(true),
    AUTHORIZATION_REQUEST(true),
    PERMISSION_ADJUSTMENT(true),
    STORED_FILE(true),
    REQUEST(false),
    OPERATION(false),
    LOGIN_ATTEMPT(false);

    private final boolean hasObject;

    AuditSubjectKind(boolean hasObject) {
        this.hasObject = hasObject;
    }

    public boolean hasObject() {
        return hasObject;
    }

    /** The kind of an event's aggregate type (UserAccount gives USER_ACCOUNT); an aggregate type with no kind is refused, never guessed. */
    public static AuditSubjectKind ofAggregateType(String aggregateType) {
        String name = aggregateType.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (AuditSubjectKind kind : values()) {
            if (kind.hasObject && kind.name().equals(name)) {
                return kind;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("aggregate type " + aggregateType + " has no audit subject kind");
    }
}

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.LegalEntityId;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgStructureCommandId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.CountersignReadViews.BasisLine;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.CountersignReadViews.Person;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.CountersignReadQueries;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Stream;
import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

/** The people a command changes the cost legal entity of, before and after; finance heads of an entity it asks and the org data administrator read it (#910 amendment B). */
@Service
public class ListAttributionCountersignBases {
    static final String LIST = "attribution-countersign-bases";

    private final CountersignReadAccess access;
    private final CountersignReadQueries names;

    ListAttributionCountersignBases(CountersignReadAccess access, CountersignReadQueries names) {
        this.access = access;
        this.names = names;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CursorPage<BasisLine> execute(OrgStructureCommandId command, @Nullable Integer limit, @Nullable String cursor) {
        PageRequest page = PageRequest.of(limit, cursor, LIST);
        access.requireBasisReadable(command);
        List<CountersignReadQueries.BasisEntry> entries = names.basisPage(command, page.cursorUuid(), page.limit() + 1);
        boolean more = entries.size() > page.limit();
        List<CountersignReadQueries.BasisEntry> shown = more ? entries.subList(0, page.limit()) : entries;
        Map<EmployeeId, CountersignReadQueries.Person> people = names.employees(shown.stream().map(e -> e.basis().employeeId()).toList());
        Map<LegalEntityId, String> entities = names.legalEntityNames(shown.stream().flatMap(e -> Stream.of(e.basis().previous(), e.basis().next())).toList());
        List<BasisLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        for (CountersignReadQueries.BasisEntry entry : shown) {
            CountersignReadQueries.Person person = people.get(entry.basis().employeeId());
            lines.add(new BasisLine(entry.basis().employeeId().value(), person == null ? "" : person.displayName(), person == null ? "" : person.employeeCode(),
                    entry.basis().previous().value(), entities.get(entry.basis().previous()), entry.basis().next().value(), entities.get(entry.basis().next())));
        }
        return CursorPage.of(lines, more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().rowKey().toString()) : null);
    }
}

    protected void seedOrganisation() throws Exception {
        try (Connection workforce = schemaConnection("organization_workforce")) {
            for (Post post : positions.values()) {
                write(workforce, "insert into positions (id, code, name, sort_order, status) values (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIVE')", post.id(), post.code(),
                        positionName(post.code()), 1);
            }
            for (Unit unit : units.values()) {
                write(workforce, "insert into org_units (id, code, name, parent_org_unit_id, sort_order, status) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIVE')",
                        stored(unit.id()), unit.code(), unitName(unit.code()), unit.parentCode() == null ? null : stored(known(units, unit.parentCode()).id()),
                        unit.sortOrder());
            }
            for (Person person : people.values()) {
                write(workforce, """
                        insert into employees (id, employee_code, display_name, login_name, email, gender, wecom_user_id, employment_status, is_fee_earner)
                        values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'FEMALE', ?, 'EMPLOYED', false)""", stored(person.id()), employeeCode(person.key()), "Synthetic " + person.key(),
                        "f1-login-" + person.key(), person.key() + "@example.test", wecomUserId(person.key()));
                write(workforce, "insert into assignments (employee_id, org_unit_id, position_id) values (?, ?, ?)", stored(person.id()),
                        stored(known(units, person.unitCode()).id()), known(positions, person.positionCode()).id());
            }
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> head : heads.entrySet()) {
                write(workforce, "update org_units set head_employee_id = ? where id = ?", stored(known(people, head.getValue()).id()),
                        stored(known(units, head.getKey()).id()));
            }
        }
        syncRun("SUCCEEDED", clock.instant().minusSeconds(120), clock.instant().minusSeconds(60));
    }

    private static void write(Connection connection, String sql, Object... args) throws SQLException {
        try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
                statement.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
            }
            statement.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    // ---- sessions ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static String wecomUserId(String key) {
        return "f1-wx-" + key;
    }

    /** A fresh ordinary session of the person through the WeCom login; take a new one after the person's roles changed. */
    protected String login(String key) throws Exception {
        LoginState state = loginState();
        String code = "f1-code-" + key + "-" + codes.incrementAndGet();
        when(wecomGateway.resolveWecomUserId(code, WecomEntry.WORKBENCH)).thenReturn(Optional.of(new WecomUserId(wecomUserId(key))));
        MockHttpServletResponse login = mvc.perform(post("/api/v1/platform/sessions:wecom-login").with(csrf()).cookie(new Cookie("loginBinding", state.binding()))
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).content("{\"code\":\"" + code + "\",\"state\":\"" + state.state() + "\",\"entry\":\"WORKBENCH\"}"))
                .andReturn().getResponse();
        require(login, 200, "fixture: WeCom login of " + key);
        String session = cookie(login, "SESSION");
        if (session == null) {
            throw new AssertionError("fixture: the login of " + key + " set no SESSION cookie");
        }
        return session;
    }

# Playbook: impl
You own the implementation and evidence within the card; anything outside it is not yours.
1. Restate the card's goal and done-when and the smallest change you expect; if evidence shows the card's approach can't work, stop and tell lead.
2. Read `docs/rules/coding-core.md` before writing code.
3. Settle data shapes (types, state machine, tables) before logic; compare two options for anything crossing a module boundary.
4. Commit a failing test before the implementation; commit each unit once verified.
5. Run affected tests plus `scripts/ci/light.sh`; verify behaviour at the real entry point. For each defence you add or claim, break it in a temporary copy and show its test goes red; list the table (defence | how broken | red) in the PR body.
6. Before committing, delete speculative code and ask what else this change can remove.
7. Review per CLAUDE.md (sonnet for normal cards; opus for permission, security, data scope). The verdict binds head and patch-id: content changes need a new review, a pure rebase does not.
8. Open the PR, enqueue, read back the result after merge.
9. Unsure about a business rule or acceptance treatment: stop and tell lead. Make technical choices yourself and write them in the PR.
A skipped step stays listed as `skip: <reason>`.

Report must include:
- card, PR, head
- what you did, what you chose and why
- commands run and their real output (pass/fail counts)
- review: model, head, patch-id, verdict
- open items and business questions for lead

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+public record EmployeeRosterView(UUID employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, String employmentStatus,
+                                 @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentView> assignments) {
+    static EmployeeRosterView of(EmployeeRosterQueries.Row row) {
+        return new EmployeeRosterView(row.employeeId().value(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus().name(), row.hireDate(),
+                row.assignments().stream().map(a -> new AssignmentView(a.assignmentKind().name(), a.orgUnitId().value(), a.orgUnitName(),
+                        a.positionId() == null ? null : a.positionId().value(), a.positionName(),
+                        a.reportsToEmployeeId() == null ? null : a.reportsToEmployeeId().value(), a.reportsToDisplayName())).toList());
+    }
+
+    public record AssignmentView(String assignmentKind, UUID orgUnitId, String orgUnitName, @Nullable UUID positionId,
+                                 @Nullable String positionName, @Nullable UUID reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e3fa77a2261e2ca7dfe0c410844cd6d97b38cf75

@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries.Visible;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+
+@Service
+class EmployeeVisibility {
+    private static final String EMPLOYEE_READ = "organization.workforce:employee:read";
+    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
+    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
+
+    EmployeeVisibility(OrgUnitAccess access, OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots) {
+        this.access = access;
+        this.snapshots = snapshots;
+    }
+
+    Visible forList() {
+        OrgUnitAccess.Visible visible = access.visibleOrgUnits(EMPLOYEE_READ);
+        return new Visible(visible.everything(), visible.everything() ? Set.of() : OrgUnitVisibility.idsIn(snapshots.findAllOrgUnits(), visible));
+    }
+
+    Visible forDetail() {
+        try {
+            return forList();
+        } catch (DomainException unavailable) {
+            if (unavailable.kind() != ErrorKind.FORBIDDEN) {
+                throw unavailable;
+            }
+            return new Visible(false, Set.of());
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/application/GetEmployee.java b/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/application/GetEmployee.java
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..9cf5ef88cebe701d4ef13133b4bf3aa17bf91939

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubjectKind;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class GetEmployee {
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+    private final AuditLog audit;
+    private final CurrentUser currentUser;
+
+    GetEmployee(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility, AuditLog audit, CurrentUser currentUser) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+        this.audit = audit;
+        this.currentUser = currentUser;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public Optional<EmployeeRosterView> execute(EmployeeId employee) {
+        var row = queries.find(visibility.forDetail(), employee);
+        if (row.isEmpty() && queries.exists(employee)) {
+            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.employee.denied", currentUser.userId(), null,
+                    AuditSubject.of(AuditSubjectKind.EMPLOYEE, employee.value()), "outside the scope of the caller"));
+        }
+        return row.map(EmployeeRosterView::of);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+
+@Service
+public class GetPosition {
+    private final PositionQueries queries;
+
+    GetPosition(PositionQueries queries) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+    }
+
+    public Optional<PositionView> execute(PositionId position) {
+        return queries.find(position).map(PositionView::of);
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListEmployees {
+    private static final String LIST = "employees";
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+
+    ListEmployees(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<EmployeeRosterView> execute(@Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        List<EmployeeRosterQueries.Row> rows = queries.list(visibility.forList(), orgUnit, status, after == null ? null : new EmployeeId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(EmployeeRosterView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().employeeId().toString()) : null);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListPositions {
+    private final PositionQueries queries;
+
+    ListPositions(PositionQueries queries) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<PositionView> execute(@Nullable PositionStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        var rows = queries.list(status, after == null ? null : new PositionId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(PositionView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode("positions", shown.getLast().positionId().toString()) : null);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+public record PositionView(UUID positionId, String code, String name, String status, LocalDate effectiveFromDate,
+                           @Nullable LocalDate effectiveToDate) {
+    static PositionView of(PositionQueries.Row row) {
+        return new PositionView(row.positionId().value(), row.code(), row.name(), row.status().name(), row.effectiveFromDate(), row.effectiveToDate());
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+/** The roster as of a business day; visibility and filters are applied before paging. */
+public interface EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee);
+
+    boolean exists(EmployeeId employee);
+
+    record Visible(boolean everything, Set<OrgUnitId> orgUnits) {
+        public Visible {
+            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record Row(EmployeeId employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, EmploymentStatus employmentStatus,
+               @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentRow> assignments) {
+        public Row {
+            assignments = List.copyOf(assignments);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind assignmentKind, OrgUnitId orgUnitId, String orgUnitName,
+                         @Nullable PositionId positionId, @Nullable String positionName,
+                         @Nullable EmployeeId reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }
+}

+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+/** Company reference data, read from the non-superseded version in force today. */
+public interface PositionQueries {
+    List<Row> list(@Nullable PositionStatus status, @Nullable PositionId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(PositionId position);
+
+    record Row(PositionId positionId, String code, String name, PositionStatus status, LocalDate effectiveFromDate,
+               @Nullable LocalDate effectiveToDate) {
+    }

+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListEmployees {
+    private static final String LIST = "employees";
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+
+    ListEmployees(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<EmployeeRosterView> execute(@Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        List<EmployeeRosterQueries.Row> rows = queries.list(visibility.forList(), orgUnit, status, after == null ? null : new EmployeeId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(EmployeeRosterView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().employeeId().toString()) : null);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;

+/** The roster as of a business day; visibility and filters are applied before paging. */
+public interface EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee);
+
+    boolean exists(EmployeeId employee);
+

+        public Visible {
+            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record Row(EmployeeId employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, EmploymentStatus employmentStatus,
+               @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentRow> assignments) {
+        public Row {
+            assignments = List.copyOf(assignments);
+        }
+    }

+                         @Nullable PositionId positionId, @Nullable String positionName,
+                         @Nullable EmployeeId reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }
