+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.AssignmentKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.HashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries implements EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    /** As in OrgSnapshotRows, only employees without any versions may still be read from the snapshot during migration. */
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with employee_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name,
+                       case when v.versioned then ev.employment_status else e.employment_status end as employment_status,
+                       ev.employment_start_date as hire_date
+                  from organization_workforce.employees e
+                  cross join lateral (select exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = e.id) as versioned) v
+                  left join organization_workforce.employee_versions ev on v.versioned and ev.employee_id = e.id and ev.superseded_at is null
+                       and ev.effective_from_date <= :day and (ev.effective_to_date is null or :day < ev.effective_to_date)
+                 where not v.versioned or ev.employee_version_id is not null
+            ), assignment_rows as (
+                select av.employee_id, av.assignment_kind, av.org_unit_id, av.position_id, av.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignment_versions av
+                 where av.superseded_at is null and av.effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (av.effective_to_date is null or :day < av.effective_to_date)
+                union all
+                select a.employee_id, 'PRIMARY', a.org_unit_id, a.position_id, a.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignments a
+                 where not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+                   and not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.assignment_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+            )
+            """;
+
+    private static final String VISIBLE = """
+            , visible_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.employment_status, e.hire_date
+                  from employee_rows e
+                 where (cast(:status as text) is null or e.employment_status = :status)
+                   and ((:everything and cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null) or exists (
+                       select 1 from assignment_rows a where a.employee_id = e.id
+                         and (:everything or a.org_unit_id = any(:units))
+                         and (cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null or a.org_unit_id = :orgUnit)))
+            )
+            select id, employee_code, display_name, employment_status, hire_date from visible_rows
+            """;
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(visible, orgUnit, status, day, """
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or employee_code > (select employee_code from visible_rows where id = :after)
+                 order by employee_code limit :limit
+                """)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit)
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(visible, null, null, day, " where id = :id")
+                .param("id", employee.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day).stream().findFirst();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public boolean exists(EmployeeId employee) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + "select exists(select 1 from employee_rows where id = :id)")
+                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", employee.value()).query(Boolean.class).single();
+    }
+
+    private JdbcClient.StatementSpec visibleQuery(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, LocalDate day, String suffix) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + suffix).param("day", day).param("everything", visible.everything())
+                .param("units", visible.orgUnits().stream().map(OrgUnitId::value).toArray(UUID[]::new))
+                .param("orgUnit", orgUnit == null ? null : orgUnit.value(), Types.OTHER)
+                .param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR);
+    }
+
+    /** Fetch all assignments only for the employees already selected by the scoped SQL page. */
+    private List<Row> withAssignments(List<Row> rows, LocalDate day) {
+        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
+            return List.of();
+        }
+        Map<EmployeeId, List<AssignmentRow>> assignments = new HashMap<>();
+        jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                select a.employee_id, a.assignment_kind, a.org_unit_id, coalesce(uv.name, u.name) as org_unit_name,
+                       a.position_id, coalesce(pv.name, p.name) as position_name,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then a.reports_to_employee_id end as reports_to_employee_id,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then manager.display_name end as reports_to_display_name
+                  from assignment_rows a
+                  join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = a.org_unit_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions uv on uv.org_unit_id = u.id and uv.superseded_at is null
+                       and uv.effective_from_date <= :day and (uv.effective_to_date is null or :day < uv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.positions p on p.id = a.position_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.position_versions pv on pv.position_id = p.id and pv.superseded_at is null
+                       and pv.effective_from_date <= :day and (pv.effective_to_date is null or :day < pv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.employees manager on manager.id = a.reports_to_employee_id
+                 where a.employee_id in (:ids)
+                 order by a.assignment_kind desc, u.code, p.code
+                """).param("day", day).param("ids", rows.stream().map(row -> row.employeeId().value()).toList())
+                .query((rs, n) -> {
+                    EmployeeId employee = new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("employee_id", UUID.class));
+                    assignments.computeIfAbsent(employee, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(assignment(rs));
+                    return employee;
+                }).list();
+        return rows.stream().map(row -> new Row(row.employeeId(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus(), row.hireDate(),
+                assignments.getOrDefault(row.employeeId(), List.of()))).toList();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("employee_code"), rs.getString("display_name"),
+                EmploymentStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("employment_status")), rs.getObject("hire_date", LocalDate.class), List.of());
+    }
+
+    private static AssignmentRow assignment(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        UUID position = rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class);
+        UUID manager = rs.getObject("reports_to_employee_id", UUID.class);
+        return new AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind.valueOf(rs.getString("assignment_kind")), new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("org_unit_id", UUID.class)),
+                rs.getString("org_unit_name"), position == null ? null : new PositionId(position), rs.getString("position_name"),
+                manager == null ? null : new EmployeeId(manager), rs.getString("reports_to_display_name"));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcPositionQueries implements PositionQueries {
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with current_positions as (
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date
+                  from organization_workforce.position_versions
+                 where superseded_at is null and effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
+            )
+            """;
+    private static final String SELECT = "select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions";
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcPositionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(@Nullable PositionStatus status, @Nullable PositionId after, int limit) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                , filtered_positions as (
+                    select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions
+                     where cast(:status as text) is null or status = :status
+                )
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from filtered_positions
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or code > (select code from filtered_positions where position_id = :after)
+                 order by code limit :limit
+                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(PositionId position) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + SELECT + " where position_id = :id").param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", position.value())
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new PositionId(rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("code"), rs.getString("name"),
+                PositionStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), rs.getObject("effective_from_date", LocalDate.class), rs.getObject("effective_to_date", LocalDate.class));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries.Visible;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+
+@Service
+class EmployeeVisibility {
+    private static final String EMPLOYEE_READ = "organization.workforce:employee:read";
+    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
+    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
+
+    EmployeeVisibility(OrgUnitAccess access, OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots) {
+        this.access = access;
+        this.snapshots = snapshots;
+    }
+
+    Visible forList() {
+        OrgUnitAccess.Visible visible = access.visibleOrgUnits(EMPLOYEE_READ);
+        return new Visible(visible.everything(), visible.everything() ? Set.of() : OrgUnitVisibility.idsIn(snapshots.findAllOrgUnits(), visible));
+    }
+
+    Visible forDetail() {
+        try {
+            return forList();
+        } catch (DomainException unavailable) {
+            if (unavailable.kind() != ErrorKind.FORBIDDEN) {
+                throw unavailable;
+            }
+            return new Visible(false, Set.of());
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubjectKind;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class GetEmployee {
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+    private final AuditLog audit;
+    private final CurrentUser currentUser;
+
+    GetEmployee(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility, AuditLog audit, CurrentUser currentUser) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+        this.audit = audit;
+        this.currentUser = currentUser;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public Optional<EmployeeRosterView> execute(EmployeeId employee) {
+        var row = queries.find(visibility.forDetail(), employee);
+        if (row.isEmpty() && queries.exists(employee)) {
+            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.employee.denied", currentUser.userId(), null,
+                    AuditSubject.of(AuditSubjectKind.EMPLOYEE, employee.value()), "outside the scope of the caller"));
+        }
+        return row.map(EmployeeRosterView::of);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListEmployees {
+    private static final String LIST = "employees";
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+
+    ListEmployees(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<EmployeeRosterView> execute(@Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        List<EmployeeRosterQueries.Row> rows = queries.list(visibility.forList(), orgUnit, status, after == null ? null : new EmployeeId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(EmployeeRosterView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().employeeId().toString()) : null);
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;

+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+/** The roster as of a business day; visibility and filters are applied before paging. */
+public interface EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee);
+
+    boolean exists(EmployeeId employee);
+
+    record Visible(boolean everything, Set<OrgUnitId> orgUnits) {
+        public Visible {
+            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record Row(EmployeeId employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, EmploymentStatus employmentStatus,
+               @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentRow> assignments) {
+        public Row {
+            assignments = List.copyOf(assignments);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind assignmentKind, OrgUnitId orgUnitId, String orgUnitName,
+                         @Nullable PositionId positionId, @Nullable String positionName,
+                         @Nullable EmployeeId reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.AssignmentKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.HashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries implements EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    /** As in OrgSnapshotRows, only employees without any versions may still be read from the snapshot during migration. */
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with employee_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name,
+                       case when v.versioned then ev.employment_status else e.employment_status end as employment_status,
+                       ev.employment_start_date as hire_date
+                  from organization_workforce.employees e
+                  cross join lateral (select exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = e.id) as versioned) v
+                  left join organization_workforce.employee_versions ev on v.versioned and ev.employee_id = e.id and ev.superseded_at is null
+                       and ev.effective_from_date <= :day and (ev.effective_to_date is null or :day < ev.effective_to_date)
+                 where not v.versioned or ev.employee_version_id is not null
+            ), assignment_rows as (
+                select av.employee_id, av.assignment_kind, av.org_unit_id, av.position_id, av.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignment_versions av
+                 where av.superseded_at is null and av.effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (av.effective_to_date is null or :day < av.effective_to_date)
+                union all
+                select a.employee_id, 'PRIMARY', a.org_unit_id, a.position_id, a.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignments a
+                 where not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+                   and not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.assignment_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+            )
+            """;
+
+    private static final String VISIBLE = """
+            , visible_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.employment_status, e.hire_date
+                  from employee_rows e
+                 where (cast(:status as text) is null or e.employment_status = :status)
+                   and ((:everything and cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null) or exists (
+                       select 1 from assignment_rows a where a.employee_id = e.id
+                         and (:everything or a.org_unit_id = any(:units))
+                         and (cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null or a.org_unit_id = :orgUnit)))
+            )
+            select id, employee_code, display_name, employment_status, hire_date from visible_rows
+            """;
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(visible, orgUnit, status, day, """
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or employee_code > (select employee_code from visible_rows where id = :after)
+                 order by employee_code limit :limit
+                """)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit)
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(visible, null, null, day, " where id = :id")
+                .param("id", employee.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day).stream().findFirst();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public boolean exists(EmployeeId employee) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + "select exists(select 1 from employee_rows where id = :id)")
+                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", employee.value()).query(Boolean.class).single();
+    }
+
+    private JdbcClient.StatementSpec visibleQuery(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, LocalDate day, String suffix) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + suffix).param("day", day).param("everything", visible.everything())
+                .param("units", visible.orgUnits().stream().map(OrgUnitId::value).toArray(UUID[]::new))
+                .param("orgUnit", orgUnit == null ? null : orgUnit.value(), Types.OTHER)
+                .param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR);
+    }
+
+    /** Fetch all assignments only for the employees already selected by the scoped SQL page. */
+    private List<Row> withAssignments(List<Row> rows, LocalDate day) {
+        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
+            return List.of();
+        }
+        Map<EmployeeId, List<AssignmentRow>> assignments = new HashMap<>();
+        jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                select a.employee_id, a.assignment_kind, a.org_unit_id, coalesce(uv.name, u.name) as org_unit_name,
+                       a.position_id, coalesce(pv.name, p.name) as position_name,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then a.reports_to_employee_id end as reports_to_employee_id,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then manager.display_name end as reports_to_display_name
+                  from assignment_rows a
+                  join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = a.org_unit_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions uv on uv.org_unit_id = u.id and uv.superseded_at is null
+                       and uv.effective_from_date <= :day and (uv.effective_to_date is null or :day < uv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.positions p on p.id = a.position_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.position_versions pv on pv.position_id = p.id and pv.superseded_at is null
+                       and pv.effective_from_date <= :day and (pv.effective_to_date is null or :day < pv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.employees manager on manager.id = a.reports_to_employee_id
+                 where a.employee_id in (:ids)
+                 order by a.assignment_kind desc, u.code, p.code
+                """).param("day", day).param("ids", rows.stream().map(row -> row.employeeId().value()).toList())
+                .query((rs, n) -> {
+                    EmployeeId employee = new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("employee_id", UUID.class));
+                    assignments.computeIfAbsent(employee, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(assignment(rs));
+                    return employee;
+                }).list();
+        return rows.stream().map(row -> new Row(row.employeeId(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus(), row.hireDate(),
+                assignments.getOrDefault(row.employeeId(), List.of()))).toList();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("employee_code"), rs.getString("display_name"),
+                EmploymentStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("employment_status")), rs.getObject("hire_date", LocalDate.class), List.of());
+    }
+
+    private static AssignmentRow assignment(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        UUID position = rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class);
+        UUID manager = rs.getObject("reports_to_employee_id", UUID.class);
+        return new AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind.valueOf(rs.getString("assignment_kind")), new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("org_unit_id", UUID.class)),
+                rs.getString("org_unit_name"), position == null ? null : new PositionId(position), rs.getString("position_name"),
+                manager == null ? null : new EmployeeId(manager), rs.getString("reports_to_display_name"));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcPositionQueries implements PositionQueries {
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with current_positions as (
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date
+                  from organization_workforce.position_versions
+                 where superseded_at is null and effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
+            )
+            """;
+    private static final String SELECT = "select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions";
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcPositionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(@Nullable PositionStatus status, @Nullable PositionId after, int limit) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                , filtered_positions as (
+                    select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions
+                     where cast(:status as text) is null or status = :status
+                )
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from filtered_positions
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or code > (select code from filtered_positions where position_id = :after)
+                 order by code limit :limit
+                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(PositionId position) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + SELECT + " where position_id = :id").param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", position.value())
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new PositionId(rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("code"), rs.getString("name"),
+                PositionStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), rs.getObject("effective_from_date", LocalDate.class), rs.getObject("effective_to_date", LocalDate.class));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.web;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.WorkforceError;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.GetEmployee;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.ListEmployees;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
+
+@RestController
+@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization/employees")
+class EmployeeController {
+    private final ListEmployees list;
+    private final GetEmployee get;
+
+    EmployeeController(ListEmployees list, GetEmployee get) {
+        this.list = list;
+        this.get = get;
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    CursorPage<EmployeeRosterResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable UUID orgUnitId,
+                                           @RequestParam(defaultValue = "EMPLOYED") EmploymentFilter employmentStatus,
+                                           @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable Integer limit, @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable String cursor) {
+        EmploymentStatus status = switch (employmentStatus) {
+            case EMPLOYED -> EmploymentStatus.EMPLOYED;
+            case SEPARATED -> EmploymentStatus.SEPARATED;
+            case ALL -> null;
+        };
+        var page = list.execute(orgUnitId == null ? null : new OrgUnitId(orgUnitId), status, PageRequest.of(limit, cursor, "employees"));
+        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(EmployeeRosterResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping("/{employeeId}")
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    EmployeeDetailResponse get(@PathVariable UUID employeeId) {
+        return get.execute(new EmployeeId(employeeId)).map(EmployeeDetailResponse::of).orElseThrow(() -> new DomainException(
+                WorkforceError.ORGANIZATION_WORKFORCE_EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND, ErrorKind.NOT_FOUND, "no employee " + employeeId));
+    }
+
+    enum EmploymentFilter {
+        EMPLOYED, ALL, SEPARATED
+    }
+}

-- IMP-1022-PAR-03（#711，one-design 签发 #711 评论修订 2 及用户答复「1a 2a 3a」）：任职版本化，主任职不重叠、兼职不限。
-- 本迁移只改表结构与约束：任职根表、版本与人事命令对任职根的引用、新增兼职与结束兼职两种命令。应用代码写入主任职根（保证既有写入方在新约束下照常写入），并在开始兼职命令生效时写入兼职任职根。

-- 1. 任职根：每个员工一条主任职根（终身一条），兼职每个一条；只追加。
CREATE TABLE organization_workforce.employee_assignments (
    assignment_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    employee_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    assignment_kind text        NOT NULL,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_employee_assignments PRIMARY KEY (assignment_id),
    CONSTRAINT uk_employee_assignments_identity UNIQUE (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind),
    CONSTRAINT fk_employee_assignments_employee_id FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES organization_workforce.employees (id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT ck_employee_assignments_assignment_kind CHECK (assignment_kind IN ('PRIMARY', 'CONCURRENT'))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_employee_assignments_primary ON organization_workforce.employee_assignments (employee_id) WHERE assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY';
CREATE INDEX ix_employee_assignments_employee_id ON organization_workforce.employee_assignments (employee_id);
COMMENT ON TABLE organization_workforce.employee_assignments IS '任职根：员工的一个任职（主任职或兼职），版本与人事命令都按它归属；主任职每个员工终身一条，兼职不限；只追加；粒度：每个员工的每个任职一行';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.employee_assignments.assignment_id IS '任职 ID（UUIDv7）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.employee_assignments.employee_id IS '员工 ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.employee_assignments.assignment_kind IS '任职类别：PRIMARY 主任职 / CONCURRENT 兼职；复合外键把它抄到版本与命令上，使检查约束与部分唯一索引能直接判断';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.employee_assignments.created_at IS '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.employee_assignments.updated_at IS '更新时间';

CREATE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_employee_assignment_rewrite() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp AS $$
DECLARE
    owner_name name;
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'TRUNCATE' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'employee assignments are append-only: truncate on %.% is refused', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        SELECT pg_get_userbyid(relowner) INTO owner_name FROM pg_class WHERE oid = TG_RELID;
        IF current_user <> owner_name THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'employee assignments are append-only: delete on %.% is refused', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
        END IF;
        RETURN OLD;
    END IF;
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'employee assignments are append-only: update on %.% is refused', TG_TABLE_SCHEMA, TG_TABLE_NAME USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
END $$;
COMMENT ON FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_employee_assignment_rewrite() IS '任职根只追加的触发器：任何 UPDATE 拒绝，DELETE 只放行表属主（清除通道随 W2 第 8 节另批），TRUNCATE 拒绝';
CREATE TRIGGER tg_employee_assignments_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON organization_workforce.employee_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_employee_assignment_rewrite();
CREATE TRIGGER tg_employee_assignments_append_only_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON organization_workforce.employee_assignments
    FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_employee_assignment_rewrite();

-- 2. 已有数据：有任职版本或人事命令的员工各补一条主任职根；补写列要在只追加触发器拆掉之后、重建之前，触发器不放行这些列的改动。
INSERT INTO organization_workforce.employee_assignments (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind, created_at, updated_at)
SELECT uuidv7(), e.employee_id, 'PRIMARY', now(), now()
  FROM (SELECT employee_id FROM organization_workforce.assignment_versions UNION SELECT employee_id FROM organization_workforce.personnel_commands) e;

ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions ADD COLUMN assignment_id uuid, ADD COLUMN assignment_kind text;
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.personnel_commands ADD COLUMN target_assignment_id uuid, ADD COLUMN target_assignment_kind text;
DROP TRIGGER tg_assignment_versions_append_only ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions;
DROP TRIGGER tg_assignment_versions_append_only_truncate ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions;
DROP TRIGGER tg_personnel_commands_append_only ON organization_workforce.personnel_commands;
DROP TRIGGER tg_personnel_commands_append_only_truncate ON organization_workforce.personnel_commands;
UPDATE organization_workforce.assignment_versions v SET assignment_id = r.assignment_id, assignment_kind = r.assignment_kind
  FROM organization_workforce.employee_assignments r WHERE r.employee_id = v.employee_id AND r.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY';
UPDATE organization_workforce.personnel_commands c SET target_assignment_id = r.assignment_id, target_assignment_kind = r.assignment_kind
  FROM organization_workforce.employee_assignments r
 WHERE r.employee_id = c.employee_id AND r.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY'
   AND (c.command_kind = 'TRANSFER' OR (c.command_kind = 'CORRECTION' AND num_nonnulls(c.target_org_unit_id, c.target_position_id, c.target_reports_to_employee_id) > 0));
CREATE TRIGGER tg_assignment_versions_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_assignment_version_rewrite();
CREATE TRIGGER tg_assignment_versions_append_only_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions
    FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_assignment_version_rewrite();
CREATE TRIGGER tg_personnel_commands_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON organization_workforce.personnel_commands
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_personnel_command_rewrite();
CREATE TRIGGER tg_personnel_commands_append_only_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON organization_workforce.personnel_commands
    FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION organization_workforce.refuse_personnel_command_rewrite();

-- 3. 任职版本：挂在任职根上；同一任职内不重叠（主任职只有一条根，正是 INV-62），兼职之间与对主任职都可并行；兼职没有汇报线；更正留在自己的任职上。
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions
    ALTER COLUMN assignment_id SET NOT NULL,
    ALTER COLUMN assignment_kind SET NOT NULL,
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_assignment_versions_assignment FOREIGN KEY (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind)
        REFERENCES organization_workforce.employee_assignments (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_assignment_versions_assignment_kind CHECK (assignment_kind IN ('PRIMARY', 'CONCURRENT')),
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_assignment_versions_concurrent_reports_to CHECK (assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' OR reports_to_employee_id IS NULL),
    ADD CONSTRAINT uk_assignment_versions_id_assignment UNIQUE (assignment_version_id, assignment_id);
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions DROP CONSTRAINT ex_assignment_versions_overlap;
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions
    ADD CONSTRAINT ex_assignment_versions_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (assignment_id WITH =, daterange(effective_from_date, effective_to_date) WITH &&) WHERE (superseded_at IS NULL);
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions DROP CONSTRAINT fk_assignment_versions_corrected_from_version_id;
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_assignment_versions_corrected_from FOREIGN KEY (corrected_from_version_id, assignment_id)
        REFERENCES organization_workforce.assignment_versions (assignment_version_id, assignment_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;
DROP INDEX organization_workforce.ix_assignment_versions_corrected_from_version_id;
CREATE INDEX ix_assignment_versions_corrected_from ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions (corrected_from_version_id, assignment_id) WHERE corrected_from_version_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_assignment_versions_assignment_id ON organization_workforce.assignment_versions (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind);
COMMENT ON TABLE organization_workforce.assignment_versions IS '任职版本：员工一个任职（主任职或兼职）所在部门、岗位与汇报线的变化历史，只追加（开放版本只能被关闭，同一天的再次变化关闭当天版本并追加下一修订；作废只能由空变非空一次）；生效依据为人事业务生效日（DECLARED）；粒度：每个任职的每个生效区间每个修订一行；主任职内不重叠，兼职可与任何任职并行；与快照在同一事务写入';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.assignment_versions.assignment_id IS '任职 ID：本版本所属的任职根（employee_assignments）；更正重新追加的行与被更正的行属于同一任职';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.assignment_versions.assignment_kind IS '任职类别：抄自任职根，由复合外键保证相等；兼职版本没有直属领导（检查约束 ck_assignment_versions_concurrent_reports_to）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN organization_workforce.assignment_versions.reports_to_employee_id IS '直属领导（员工 ID）；只有主任职有汇报线，兼职版本必为空';

-- 4. 人事命令：命令作用的任职、新增兼职与结束兼职两种命令、按人或主任职与按兼职分别一天一条未生效命令、合并按任职各产生一条调岗。
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.personnel_commands
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_personnel_commands_target_assignment FOREIGN KEY (target_assignment_id, employee_id, target_assignment_kind)
        REFERENCES organization_workforce.employee_assignments (assignment_id, employee_id, assignment_kind) ON DELETE RESTRICT,

    ('HIRE', 'REGULARIZE', 'TRANSFER', 'SEPARATE', 'REINSTATE', 'CORRECTION', 'UNPAID_LEAVE_START', 'UNPAID_LEAVE_END', 'CHANGE_LEGAL_ENTITY', 'START_CONCURRENT', 'END_CONCURRENT'));
-- 目标任职：调岗必填；更正带任职列时必填、不带时必空；结束兼职必填且指向兼职根；其余种类（含新增兼职，它自己建根）不带。
ALTER TABLE organization_workforce.personnel_commands ADD CONSTRAINT ck_personnel_commands_target_assignment CHECK (
    CASE
        WHEN command_kind = 'TRANSFER' THEN target_assignment_id IS NOT NULL
        WHEN command_kind = 'CORRECTION' THEN (target_assignment_id IS NOT NULL) = (num_nonnulls(target_org_unit_id, target_position_id, target_reports_to_employee_id) > 0)
        WHEN command_kind = 'END_CONCURRENT' THEN target_assignment_id IS NOT NULL AND target_assignment_kind = 'CONCURRENT'
        ELSE target_assignment_id IS NULL
    END);
-- 兼职没有汇报线与收费员工标记；新增兼职要部门与岗位；结束兼职不带别的目标列。

            AND num_nonnulls(target_reports_to_employee_id, is_target_fee_earner, target_employment_phase_kind, is_target_on_unpaid_leave) = 0

package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;

import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
import java.util.Objects;

public record Position(PositionId positionId, String code, String name, Integer sortOrder, PositionStatus status) {
    public Position {
        Objects.requireNonNull(positionId, "positionId");
        Objects.requireNonNull(name, "name");
        Objects.requireNonNull(status, "status");
    }

    public Position withStatus(PositionStatus newStatus) {
        return new Position(positionId, code, name, sortOrder, newStatus);
    }
}
package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.AttributionKind;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.WorkforceError;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.LegalEntityAttributionQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * The legal entity of a unit is read only by someone who may see the unit, and that of an employee by someone who may see the unit
 * the employee works in. Anything else answers exactly like a subject that does not exist, and is audited. version is null while nothing
 * has ever been attributed to the subject: the first attribution is then written with If-Match: "0".
 */
@Service
public class GetLegalEntityAttribution {
    private final LegalEntityAttributionQueries attributions;
    private final AttributionSubjects subjects;
    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshot;
    private final AuditLog audit;
    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
    private final CurrentUser currentUser;

    GetLegalEntityAttribution(LegalEntityAttributionQueries attributions, AttributionSubjects subjects, OrgSnapshotQueries snapshot, AuditLog audit,
            OrgUnitAccess access, CurrentUser currentUser) {
        this.access = access;
        this.currentUser = currentUser;
        this.attributions = attributions;
        this.subjects = subjects;
        this.snapshot = snapshot;
        this.audit = audit;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public LegalEntityAttributionView execute(AttributionKind kind, UUID subjectId) {
        var subject = subjects.require(kind, subjectId);
        Set<OrgUnitId> allowed = OrgUnitVisibility.idsInForDetail(snapshot.findAllOrgUnits(), access);
        boolean permitted = switch (kind) {
            case ORG_UNIT -> allowed.contains(new OrgUnitId(subjectId));
            case EMPLOYEE -> snapshot.findStaff(new EmployeeId(subjectId)).map(staff -> allowed.contains(staff.assignment().orgUnitId())).orElse(false);
        };
        if (!permitted) {
            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.legal-entity-attribution.denied", currentUser.userId(), null,
                    AuditSubject.of(AttributionAuditKinds.of(kind), subjectId), "outside the scope of the caller"));
            throw new DomainException(WorkforceError.ORGANIZATION_WORKFORCE_ATTRIBUTION_SUBJECT_NOT_FOUND, ErrorKind.NOT_FOUND,
                    "no such " + kind.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        }
        return LegalEntityAttributionView.of(subject, attributions.periodsOf(subject), attributions.versionOf(subject).orElse(null));
    }
}

71:**CS-2.20 Output ID value objects with `value()`** (new; R-1a review (PR #125 body) H-1)
72:- When a typed ID (`@ValueObject` records such as `UserId`, `OrgUnitId`, `SyncRunId`) is written to an interface response, event payload, log key, cursor, audit detail or SQL parameter, always take `id.value()` (when text is needed, `id.value().toString()` or `String.valueOf(id.value())`); **never call `String.valueOf(id)` / `id.toString()` or string concatenation on the ID object itself**: a record's `toString()` is `OrgUnitId[value=…]`, and changing the implementation (long → UUID) would silently change the external shape.
78:**CS-2.21 Do not express business order by ID order** (new; R-1a review (PR #125 body) H-2)
79:- UUIDv7 roughly increases with time and auto-increment IDs with insertion, but **neither is a business fact**: same-millisecond ids, clock rollback, bulk import and migrated-in data all break it. When order is needed, use an explicit sequence column (`_number` / `_order`, allocated in the same transaction) or "business time + `id`": business time decides the order, `id` is only a stable tie-breaker (the same for the sort key of cursor pagination, NAMING_STANDARD §2.4a item 2).
80:- Forbidden: `ORDER BY id` to express "earlier/later", `max(id)` as "latest", comparing two IDs to decide which came first, tests whose assertions depend on ID order.
131:**CS-2.29 The domain layer does not reference external-system types** (new): external-origin types (`Source*` in `platform.integration.api`, e.g. `SourceUnitId`, `SourcePersonId`, `SourceOrgSnapshot`) appear only in `platform.integration` and at the consuming module's **anti-corruption translation point**; a business module's aggregates, entities, value objects, `api` and `events` neither hold nor expose them, and after translation use only their own types and enums (DATA_DESIGN_CHECKLIST §14.1 D16 "external IDs serve only as source-reference columns … used only in the sync anti-corruption layer"; §14 item 8). Current state: `SourceTerms`, `SnapshotReconciler` and `EmployeeChecks` in `organization/workforce/domain/` reference `Source*`; the user must decide where the translation point goes (§10 Q-4).

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+public record EmployeeRosterView(UUID employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, String employmentStatus,
+                                 @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentView> assignments) {
+    static EmployeeRosterView of(EmployeeRosterQueries.Row row) {
+        return new EmployeeRosterView(row.employeeId().value(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus().name(), row.hireDate(),
+                row.assignments().stream().map(a -> new AssignmentView(a.assignmentKind().name(), a.orgUnitId().value(), a.orgUnitName(),
+                        a.positionId() == null ? null : a.positionId().value(), a.positionName(),
+                        a.reportsToEmployeeId() == null ? null : a.reportsToEmployeeId().value(), a.reportsToDisplayName())).toList());
+    }
+
+    public record AssignmentView(String assignmentKind, UUID orgUnitId, String orgUnitName, @Nullable UUID positionId,
+                                 @Nullable String positionName, @Nullable UUID reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries.Visible;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+
+@Service
+class EmployeeVisibility {
+    private static final String EMPLOYEE_READ = "organization.workforce:employee:read";
+    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
+    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
+
+    EmployeeVisibility(OrgUnitAccess access, OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots) {
+        this.access = access;
+        this.snapshots = snapshots;
+    }
+
+    Visible forList() {
+        OrgUnitAccess.Visible visible = access.visibleOrgUnits(EMPLOYEE_READ);
+        return new Visible(visible.everything(), visible.everything() ? Set.of() : OrgUnitVisibility.idsIn(snapshots.findAllOrgUnits(), visible));
+    }
+
+    Visible forDetail() {
+        try {
+            return forList();
+        } catch (DomainException unavailable) {
+            if (unavailable.kind() != ErrorKind.FORBIDDEN) {
+                throw unavailable;
+            }
+            return new Visible(false, Set.of());
+        }
+    }
+}

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..9cf5ef88cebe701d4ef13133b4bf3aa17bf91939

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubjectKind;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class GetEmployee {
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+    private final AuditLog audit;
+    private final CurrentUser currentUser;
+
+    GetEmployee(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility, AuditLog audit, CurrentUser currentUser) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+        this.audit = audit;
+        this.currentUser = currentUser;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public Optional<EmployeeRosterView> execute(EmployeeId employee) {
+        var row = queries.find(visibility.forDetail(), employee);
+        if (row.isEmpty() && queries.exists(employee)) {
+            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.employee.denied", currentUser.userId(), null,
+                    AuditSubject.of(AuditSubjectKind.EMPLOYEE, employee.value()), "outside the scope of the caller"));
+        }
+        return row.map(EmployeeRosterView::of);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListEmployees {
+    private static final String LIST = "employees";
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+
+    ListEmployees(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<EmployeeRosterView> execute(@Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        List<EmployeeRosterQueries.Row> rows = queries.list(visibility.forList(), orgUnit, status, after == null ? null : new EmployeeId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(EmployeeRosterView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().employeeId().value().toString()) : null);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListPositions {
+    public enum Status {
+        ACTIVE, DISABLED
+    }
+
+    private final PositionQueries queries;
+
+    ListPositions(PositionQueries queries) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<PositionView> execute(@Nullable Status status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        var rows = queries.list(status == null ? null : PositionStatus.valueOf(status.name()), after == null ? null : new PositionId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(PositionView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode("positions", shown.getLast().positionId().value().toString()) : null);
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;

+import java.util.List;

+/** The roster as of a business day; visibility and filters are applied before paging. */
+public interface EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee);
+
+    boolean exists(EmployeeId employee);
+
+    record Visible(boolean everything, Set<OrgUnitId> orgUnits) {
+        public Visible {
+            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record Row(EmployeeId employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, EmploymentStatus employmentStatus,
+               @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentRow> assignments) {
+        public Row {
+            assignments = List.copyOf(assignments);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind assignmentKind, OrgUnitId orgUnitId, String orgUnitName,
+                         @Nullable PositionId positionId, @Nullable String positionName,
+                         @Nullable EmployeeId reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }

+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.HashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries implements EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    /** As in OrgSnapshotRows, only employees without any versions may still be read from the snapshot during migration. */
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with employee_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name,
+                       case when v.versioned then ev.employment_status else e.employment_status end as employment_status,
+                       ev.employment_start_date as hire_date
+                  from organization_workforce.employees e
+                  cross join lateral (select exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = e.id) as versioned) v
+                  left join organization_workforce.employee_versions ev on v.versioned and ev.employee_id = e.id and ev.superseded_at is null
+                       and ev.effective_from_date <= :day and (ev.effective_to_date is null or :day < ev.effective_to_date)
+                 where not v.versioned or ev.employee_version_id is not null
+            ), assignment_rows as (
+                select av.employee_id, av.assignment_kind, av.org_unit_id, av.position_id, av.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignment_versions av
+                 where av.superseded_at is null and av.effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (av.effective_to_date is null or :day < av.effective_to_date)
+                union all
+                select a.employee_id, 'PRIMARY', a.org_unit_id, a.position_id, a.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignments a
+                 where not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+                   and not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.assignment_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+            )
+            """;
+
+    private static final String VISIBLE = """
+            , visible_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.employment_status, e.hire_date
+                  from employee_rows e
+                 where (cast(:status as text) is null or e.employment_status = :status)
+                   and ((:everything and cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null) or exists (
+                       select 1 from assignment_rows a where a.employee_id = e.id
+                         and (:everything or a.org_unit_id = any(:units))
+                         and (cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null or a.org_unit_id = :orgUnit)))
+            )
+            select id, employee_code, display_name, employment_status, hire_date from visible_rows
+            """;
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + """
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or employee_code > (select employee_code from visible_rows where id = :after)
+                 order by employee_code limit :limit
+                """), visible, orgUnit, status, day)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit)
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + " where id = :id"), visible, null, null, day)
+                .param("id", employee.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day).stream().findFirst();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public boolean exists(EmployeeId employee) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + "select exists(select 1 from employee_rows where id = :id)")
+                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", employee.value()).query(Boolean.class).single();
+    }
+
+    private static JdbcClient.StatementSpec visibleQuery(JdbcClient.StatementSpec query, Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit,
+                                                         @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, LocalDate day) {
+        return query.param("day", day).param("everything", visible.everything())
+                .param("units", visible.orgUnits().stream().map(OrgUnitId::value).toArray(UUID[]::new))
+                .param("orgUnit", orgUnit == null ? null : orgUnit.value(), Types.OTHER)
+                .param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR);
+    }
+
+    /** Fetch all assignments only for the employees already selected by the scoped SQL page. */
+    private List<Row> withAssignments(List<Row> rows, LocalDate day) {
+        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
+            return List.of();
+        }
+        Map<EmployeeId, List<AssignmentRow>> assignments = new HashMap<>();
+        jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                select a.employee_id, a.assignment_kind, a.org_unit_id, coalesce(uv.name, u.name) as org_unit_name,
+                       a.position_id, coalesce(pv.name, p.name) as position_name,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then a.reports_to_employee_id end as reports_to_employee_id,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then manager.display_name end as reports_to_display_name
+                  from assignment_rows a
+                  join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = a.org_unit_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions uv on uv.org_unit_id = u.id and uv.superseded_at is null
+                       and uv.effective_from_date <= :day and (uv.effective_to_date is null or :day < uv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.positions p on p.id = a.position_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.position_versions pv on pv.position_id = p.id and pv.superseded_at is null
+                       and pv.effective_from_date <= :day and (pv.effective_to_date is null or :day < pv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.employees manager on manager.id = a.reports_to_employee_id
+                 where a.employee_id in (:ids)
+                 order by a.assignment_kind desc, u.code, p.code
+                """).param("day", day).param("ids", rows.stream().map(row -> row.employeeId().value()).toList())
+                .query((rs, n) -> {
+                    EmployeeId employee = new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("employee_id", UUID.class));
+                    assignments.computeIfAbsent(employee, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(assignment(rs));
+                    return employee;
+                }).list();
+        return rows.stream().map(row -> new Row(row.employeeId(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus(), row.hireDate(),
+                assignments.getOrDefault(row.employeeId(), List.of()))).toList();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("employee_code"), rs.getString("display_name"),
+                EmploymentStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("employment_status")), rs.getObject("hire_date", LocalDate.class), List.of());
+    }
+
+    private static AssignmentRow assignment(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        UUID position = rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class);
+        UUID manager = rs.getObject("reports_to_employee_id", UUID.class);
+        return new AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind.valueOf(rs.getString("assignment_kind")), new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("org_unit_id", UUID.class)),
+                rs.getString("org_unit_name"), position == null ? null : new PositionId(position), rs.getString("position_name"),
+                manager == null ? null : new EmployeeId(manager), rs.getString("reports_to_display_name"));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcPositionQueries implements PositionQueries {
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with current_positions as (
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date
+                  from organization_workforce.position_versions
+                 where superseded_at is null and effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
+            )
+            """;
+    private static final String SELECT = "select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions";
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcPositionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(@Nullable PositionStatus status, @Nullable PositionId after, int limit) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                , filtered_positions as (
+                    select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions
+                     where cast(:status as text) is null or status = :status
+                )
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from filtered_positions
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or (coalesce(code, ''), position_id) >
+                       (select coalesce(code, ''), position_id from filtered_positions where position_id = :after)
+                 order by coalesce(code, ''), position_id limit :limit
+                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(PositionId position) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + SELECT + " where position_id = :id").param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", position.value())
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new PositionId(rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("code"), rs.getString("name"),
+                PositionStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), rs.getObject("effective_from_date", LocalDate.class), rs.getObject("effective_to_date", LocalDate.class));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.web;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;

+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.GetEmployee;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.ListEmployees;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
+
+@RestController
+@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization/employees")
+class EmployeeController {
+    private final ListEmployees list;
+    private final GetEmployee get;
+
+    EmployeeController(ListEmployees list, GetEmployee get) {
+        this.list = list;
+        this.get = get;
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    CursorPage<EmployeeRosterResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable UUID orgUnitId,
+                                           @RequestParam(defaultValue = "EMPLOYED") EmploymentFilter employmentStatus,
+                                           @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable Integer limit, @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable String cursor) {
+        EmploymentStatus status = switch (employmentStatus) {
+            case EMPLOYED -> EmploymentStatus.EMPLOYED;
+            case SEPARATED -> EmploymentStatus.SEPARATED;
+            case ALL -> null;
+        };
+        var page = list.execute(orgUnitId == null ? null : new OrgUnitId(orgUnitId), status, PageRequest.of(limit, cursor, "employees"));
+        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(EmployeeRosterResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping("/{employeeId}")
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    EmployeeDetailResponse get(@PathVariable UUID employeeId) {
+        return get.execute(new EmployeeId(employeeId)).map(EmployeeDetailResponse::of).orElseThrow(() -> new DomainException(
+                WorkforceError.ORGANIZATION_WORKFORCE_EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND, ErrorKind.NOT_FOUND, "no employee " + employeeId));
+    }
+
+    enum EmploymentFilter {
+        EMPLOYED, ALL, SEPARATED
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+public record EmployeeRosterView(UUID employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, String employmentStatus,
+                                 @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentView> assignments) {
+    static EmployeeRosterView of(EmployeeRosterQueries.Row row) {
+        return new EmployeeRosterView(row.employeeId().value(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus().name(), row.hireDate(),
+                row.assignments().stream().map(a -> new AssignmentView(a.assignmentKind().name(), a.orgUnitId().value(), a.orgUnitName(),
+                        a.positionId() == null ? null : a.positionId().value(), a.positionName(),
+                        a.reportsToEmployeeId() == null ? null : a.reportsToEmployeeId().value(), a.reportsToDisplayName())).toList());
+    }
+
+    public record AssignmentView(String assignmentKind, UUID orgUnitId, String orgUnitName, @Nullable UUID positionId,
+                                 @Nullable String positionName, @Nullable UUID reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.OrgUnitAccess;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries.Visible;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.OrgSnapshotQueries;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+
+@Service
+class EmployeeVisibility {
+    private static final String EMPLOYEE_READ = "organization.workforce:employee:read";
+    private final OrgUnitAccess access;
+    private final OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots;
+
+    EmployeeVisibility(OrgUnitAccess access, OrgSnapshotQueries snapshots) {
+        this.access = access;
+        this.snapshots = snapshots;
+    }
+
+    Visible forList() {
+        OrgUnitAccess.Visible visible = access.visibleOrgUnits(EMPLOYEE_READ);
+        return new Visible(visible.everything(), visible.everything() ? Set.of() : OrgUnitVisibility.idsIn(snapshots.findAllOrgUnits(), visible));
+    }
+
+    Visible forDetail() {
+        try {
+            return forList();
+        } catch (DomainException unavailable) {
+            if (unavailable.kind() != ErrorKind.FORBIDDEN) {
+                throw unavailable;
+            }
+            return new Visible(false, Set.of());
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CurrentUser;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditEntry;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditLog;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubject;
+import com.forcitis.one.platform.audit.api.AuditSubjectKind;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class GetEmployee {
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+    private final AuditLog audit;
+    private final CurrentUser currentUser;
+
+    GetEmployee(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility, AuditLog audit, CurrentUser currentUser) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+        this.audit = audit;
+        this.currentUser = currentUser;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public Optional<EmployeeRosterView> execute(EmployeeId employee) {
+        var row = queries.find(visibility.forDetail(), employee);
+        if (row.isEmpty() && queries.exists(employee)) {
+            audit.recordIndependently(new AuditEntry("organization.workforce.employee.denied", currentUser.userId(), null,
+                    AuditSubject.of(AuditSubjectKind.EMPLOYEE, employee.value()), "outside the scope of the caller"));
+        }
+        return row.map(EmployeeRosterView::of);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListEmployees {
+    private static final String LIST = "employees";
+    private final EmployeeRosterQueries queries;
+    private final EmployeeVisibility visibility;
+
+    ListEmployees(EmployeeRosterQueries queries, EmployeeVisibility visibility) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+        this.visibility = visibility;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<EmployeeRosterView> execute(@Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        List<EmployeeRosterQueries.Row> rows = queries.list(visibility.forList(), orgUnit, status, after == null ? null : new EmployeeId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(EmployeeRosterView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode(LIST, shown.getLast().employeeId().value().toString()) : null);
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application;

+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
