+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
+import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
+
+@Service
+public class ListPositions {
+    public enum Status {
+        ACTIVE, DISABLED
+    }
+
+    private final PositionQueries queries;
+
+    ListPositions(PositionQueries queries) {
+        this.queries = queries;
+    }
+
+    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
+    public CursorPage<PositionView> execute(@Nullable Status status, PageRequest page) {
+        UUID after = page.cursorUuid();
+        var rows = queries.list(status == null ? null : PositionStatus.valueOf(status.name()), after == null ? null : new PositionId(after), page.limit() + 1);
+        boolean more = rows.size() > page.limit();
+        var shown = more ? rows.subList(0, page.limit()) : rows;
+        return CursorPage.of(shown.stream().map(PositionView::of).toList(),
+                more ? PageRequest.encode("positions", shown.getLast().positionId().value().toString()) : null);
+    }

diff --git a/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/domain/EmployeeRosterQueries.java b/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/domain/EmployeeRosterQueries.java

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.Set;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+
+/** The roster as of a business day; visibility and filters are applied before paging. */
+public interface EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit);
+
+    Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee);
+
+    boolean exists(EmployeeId employee);
+
+    record Visible(boolean everything, Set<OrgUnitId> orgUnits) {
+        public Visible {
+            orgUnits = Set.copyOf(orgUnits);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record Row(EmployeeId employeeId, String employeeCode, String displayName, EmploymentStatus employmentStatus,
+               @Nullable LocalDate hireDate, List<AssignmentRow> assignments) {
+        public Row {
+            assignments = List.copyOf(assignments);
+        }
+    }
+
+    record AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind assignmentKind, OrgUnitId orgUnitId, String orgUnitName,
+                         @Nullable PositionId positionId, @Nullable String positionName,
+                         @Nullable EmployeeId reportsToEmployeeId, @Nullable String reportsToDisplayName) {
+    }
+}

+++ b/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/infrastructure/persistence/JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries.java

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.AssignmentKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.EmployeeRosterQueries;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.HashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries implements EmployeeRosterQueries {
+    /** As in OrgSnapshotRows, only employees without any versions may still be read from the snapshot during migration. */
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with employee_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name,
+                       case when v.versioned then ev.employment_status else e.employment_status end as employment_status,
+                       ev.employment_start_date as hire_date
+                  from organization_workforce.employees e
+                  cross join lateral (select exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = e.id) as versioned) v
+                  left join organization_workforce.employee_versions ev on v.versioned and ev.employee_id = e.id and ev.superseded_at is null
+                       and ev.effective_from_date <= :day and (ev.effective_to_date is null or :day < ev.effective_to_date)
+                 where not v.versioned or ev.employee_version_id is not null
+            ), assignment_rows as (
+                select av.employee_id, av.assignment_kind, av.org_unit_id, av.position_id, av.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignment_versions av
+                 where av.superseded_at is null and av.effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (av.effective_to_date is null or :day < av.effective_to_date)
+                union all
+                select a.employee_id, 'PRIMARY', a.org_unit_id, a.position_id, a.reports_to_employee_id
+                  from organization_workforce.assignments a
+                 where not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.employee_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+                   and not exists (select 1 from organization_workforce.assignment_versions x where x.employee_id = a.employee_id)
+            )
+            """;
+
+    private static final String VISIBLE = """
+            , visible_rows as (
+                select e.id, e.employee_code, e.display_name, e.employment_status, e.hire_date
+                  from employee_rows e
+                 where (cast(:status as text) is null or e.employment_status = :status)
+                   and ((:everything and cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null) or exists (
+                       select 1 from assignment_rows a where a.employee_id = e.id
+                         and (:everything or a.org_unit_id = any(:units))
+                         and (cast(:orgUnit as uuid) is null or a.org_unit_id = :orgUnit)))
+            )
+            select id, employee_code, display_name, employment_status, hire_date from visible_rows
+            """;
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcEmployeeRosterQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit, @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, @Nullable EmployeeId after, int limit) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + """
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or employee_code > (select employee_code from visible_rows where id = :after)
+                 order by employee_code limit :limit
+                """), visible, orgUnit, status, day)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit)
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(Visible visible, EmployeeId employee) {
+        LocalDate day = zone.today(clock);
+        var rows = visibleQuery(jdbc.sql(CURRENT + VISIBLE + " where id = :id"), visible, null, null, day)
+                .param("id", employee.value()).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+        return withAssignments(rows, day).stream().findFirst();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public boolean exists(EmployeeId employee) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + "select exists(select 1 from employee_rows where id = :id)")
+                .param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", employee.value()).query(Boolean.class).single();
+    }
+
+    private static JdbcClient.StatementSpec visibleQuery(JdbcClient.StatementSpec query, Visible visible, @Nullable OrgUnitId orgUnit,
+                                                         @Nullable EmploymentStatus status, LocalDate day) {
+        return query.param("day", day).param("everything", visible.everything())
+                .param("units", visible.orgUnits().stream().map(OrgUnitId::value).toArray(UUID[]::new))
+                .param("orgUnit", orgUnit == null ? null : orgUnit.value(), Types.OTHER)
+                .param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR);
+    }
+
+    /** Fetch all assignments only for the employees already selected by the scoped SQL page. */
+    private List<Row> withAssignments(List<Row> rows, LocalDate day) {
+        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
+            return List.of();
+        }
+        Map<EmployeeId, List<AssignmentRow>> assignments = new HashMap<>();
+        jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                select a.employee_id, a.assignment_kind, a.org_unit_id, coalesce(uv.name, u.name) as org_unit_name,
+                       a.position_id, coalesce(pv.name, p.name) as position_name,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then a.reports_to_employee_id end as reports_to_employee_id,
+                       case when a.assignment_kind = 'PRIMARY' then manager.display_name end as reports_to_display_name
+                  from assignment_rows a
+                  join organization_workforce.org_units u on u.id = a.org_unit_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.org_unit_versions uv on uv.org_unit_id = u.id and uv.superseded_at is null
+                       and uv.effective_from_date <= :day and (uv.effective_to_date is null or :day < uv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.positions p on p.id = a.position_id
+                  left join organization_workforce.position_versions pv on pv.position_id = p.id and pv.superseded_at is null
+                       and pv.effective_from_date <= :day and (pv.effective_to_date is null or :day < pv.effective_to_date)
+                  left join organization_workforce.employees manager on manager.id = a.reports_to_employee_id
+                 where a.employee_id in (:ids)
+                 order by a.assignment_kind desc, u.code, p.code
+                """).param("day", day).param("ids", rows.stream().map(row -> row.employeeId().value()).toList())
+                .query((rs, n) -> {
+                    EmployeeId employee = new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("employee_id", UUID.class));
+                    assignments.computeIfAbsent(employee, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(assignment(rs));
+                    return employee;
+                }).list();
+        return rows.stream().map(row -> new Row(row.employeeId(), row.employeeCode(), row.displayName(), row.employmentStatus(), row.hireDate(),
+                assignments.getOrDefault(row.employeeId(), List.of()))).toList();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new EmployeeId(rs.getObject("id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("employee_code"), rs.getString("display_name"),
+                EmploymentStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("employment_status")), rs.getObject("hire_date", LocalDate.class), List.of());
+    }
+
+    private static AssignmentRow assignment(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        UUID position = rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class);
+        UUID manager = rs.getObject("reports_to_employee_id", UUID.class);
+        return new AssignmentRow(AssignmentKind.valueOf(rs.getString("assignment_kind")), new OrgUnitId(rs.getObject("org_unit_id", UUID.class)),
+                rs.getString("org_unit_name"), position == null ? null : new PositionId(position), rs.getString("position_name"),
+                manager == null ? null : new EmployeeId(manager), rs.getString("reports_to_display_name"));
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..6eb69ec08e0ab56cf2cefdc3d9fec278008d7e9c

+++ b/backend/src/main/java/com/forcitis/one/organization/workforce/infrastructure/persistence/JdbcPositionQueries.java

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.infrastructure.persistence;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.BusinessZone;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.PositionId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionQueries;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.domain.PositionStatus;
+import java.sql.ResultSet;
+import java.sql.SQLException;
+import java.sql.Types;
+import java.time.Clock;
+import java.time.LocalDate;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Optional;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.jdbc.core.simple.JdbcClient;
+import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
+
+@Repository
+class JdbcPositionQueries implements PositionQueries {
+    private static final String CURRENT = """
+            with current_positions as (
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date
+                  from organization_workforce.position_versions
+                 where superseded_at is null and effective_from_date <= :day
+                   and (effective_to_date is null or :day < effective_to_date)
+            )
+            """;
+    private static final String SELECT = "select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions";
+
+    private final JdbcClient jdbc;
+    private final Clock clock;
+    private final BusinessZone zone;
+
+    JdbcPositionQueries(JdbcClient jdbc, Clock clock, BusinessZone zone) {
+        this.jdbc = jdbc;
+        this.clock = clock;
+        this.zone = zone;
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public List<Row> list(@Nullable PositionStatus status, @Nullable PositionId after, int limit) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + """
+                , filtered_positions as (
+                    select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from current_positions
+                     where cast(:status as text) is null or status = :status
+                )
+                select position_id, code, name, status, effective_from_date, effective_to_date from filtered_positions
+                 where cast(:after as uuid) is null or (coalesce(code, ''), position_id) >
+                       (select coalesce(code, ''), position_id from filtered_positions where position_id = :after)
+                 order by coalesce(code, ''), position_id limit :limit
+                """).param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("status", status == null ? null : status.name(), Types.VARCHAR)
+                .param("after", after == null ? null : after.value(), Types.OTHER).param("limit", limit).query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).list();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public Optional<Row> find(PositionId position) {
+        return jdbc.sql(CURRENT + SELECT + " where position_id = :id").param("day", zone.today(clock)).param("id", position.value())
+                .query((rs, n) -> row(rs)).optional();
+    }
+
+    private static Row row(ResultSet rs) throws SQLException {
+        return new Row(new PositionId(rs.getObject("position_id", UUID.class)), rs.getString("code"), rs.getString("name"),
+                PositionStatus.valueOf(rs.getString("status")), rs.getObject("effective_from_date", LocalDate.class), rs.getObject("effective_to_date", LocalDate.class));
+    }
+}

+package com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.web;
+
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.CursorPage;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.DomainException;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.EmployeeId;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.ErrorKind;
+import com.forcitis.one.kernel.PageRequest;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.EmploymentStatus;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.OrgUnitId;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.api.WorkforceError;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.GetEmployee;
+import com.forcitis.one.organization.workforce.application.ListEmployees;
+import java.util.UUID;
+import org.jspecify.annotations.Nullable;
+import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
+import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
+
+@RestController
+@RequestMapping("/api/v1/organization/employees")
+class EmployeeController {
+    private final ListEmployees list;
+    private final GetEmployee get;
+
+    EmployeeController(ListEmployees list, GetEmployee get) {
+        this.list = list;
+        this.get = get;
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    CursorPage<EmployeeRosterResponse> list(@RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable UUID orgUnitId,
+                                           @RequestParam(defaultValue = "EMPLOYED") EmploymentFilter employmentStatus,
+                                           @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable Integer limit, @RequestParam(required = false) @Nullable String cursor) {
+        EmploymentStatus status = switch (employmentStatus) {
+            case EMPLOYED -> EmploymentStatus.EMPLOYED;
+            case SEPARATED -> EmploymentStatus.SEPARATED;
+            case ALL -> null;
+        };
+        var page = list.execute(orgUnitId == null ? null : new OrgUnitId(orgUnitId), status, PageRequest.of(limit, cursor, "employees"));
+        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(EmployeeRosterResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
+    }
+
+    @GetMapping("/{employeeId}")
+    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('" + WorkforcePermissions.EMPLOYEE_READ + "')")
+    EmployeeDetailResponse get(@PathVariable UUID employeeId) {
+        return get.execute(new EmployeeId(employeeId)).map(EmployeeDetailResponse::of).orElseThrow(() -> new DomainException(
+                WorkforceError.ORGANIZATION_WORKFORCE_EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND, ErrorKind.NOT_FOUND, "no employee " + employeeId));
+    }
+
+    enum EmploymentFilter {
+        EMPLOYED, ALL, SEPARATED
+    }
+}

+        return CursorPage.of(page.items().stream().map(PositionResponse::of).toList(), page.nextCursor());
